The proposed training scheme enables more effective integration of structural aerial priors. On the nuScenes geographic split, AerialFusionMapNet achieves up to 54.7 mAP, improving over prior aerial-onboard fusion baselines from 48.8 mAP by +5.9 absolute and +12.1% relative. The results suggest that structured training design, rather than increased architectural complexity, plays a more decisive role in unlocking the full potential of aerial imagery for online HD map construction. Code and trained models are available at https://github.com/DriverlessMobility/AerialFusionMapNet.

</details>


### [107] [FLUX3D: High-Fidelity 3D Gaussian Generation with Diffusion-Aligned Sparse Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24874)
*Haorui Ji,Weizhe Liu,Hongdong Li,Hengkai Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLUX3D通过扩散对齐结构化潜在表示（DA-SLAT）和稀疏结构感知扩散框架（含SMDiT和MARoPE），解决了稀疏体素表示中2D特征判别性与3D重建保真度之间的表征瓶颈，以及生成阶段2D图像token与3D体素潜在表示之间的跨模态对齐瓶颈，在图像到3D高斯泼溅生成任务上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于稀疏体素的图像到3D高斯泼溅生成方法存在两个结构性瓶颈：一是使用面向语义抽象的判别式2D特征构建稀疏体素潜在表示，抑制了重建线索，导致表征瓶颈；二是标准扩散Transformer缺乏有效机制对齐密集2D图像token与稀疏3D体素潜在表示，导致跨模态对齐瓶颈。

Method: 提出FLUX3D框架：1）重新审视稀疏体素3D表示学习中的2D特征选择，提出扩散对齐结构化潜在表示（DA-SLAT），配合纯解码器架构提升3DGS重建保真度；2）设计稀疏结构感知扩散框架，包含稀疏结构多模态扩散Transformer（SMDiT）和模态感知旋转位置编码（MARoPE），实现几何无关的2D-3D对齐。

Result: 在大量基准实验上，FLUX3D在外观保真度上取得显著提升，在所有生成高质量3DGS资产的最先进方法中表现最优。

Conclusion: FLUX3D通过解决表征瓶颈和跨模态对齐瓶颈，有效提升了图像到3DGS生成的质量，验证了扩散对齐结构化潜在表示与稀疏结构感知扩散框架的有效性。

Abstract: Sparse voxel representation has emerged as a scalable foundation for image-to-3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) generation, yet current methods struggle to preserve high-frequency visual details of input images due to two structural bottlenecks. First, they adopt discriminative 2D features optimized for semantic abstraction to construct sparse voxel latents, which suppress reconstructive cues and induce a representation bottleneck. Second, in the generation stage, standard diffusion transformers lack effective mechanisms to align dense 2D image tokens with sparse 3D voxel latents, resulting in a cross-modal correspondence bottleneck. To address these issues, we propose FLUX3D, a scalable image-to-3DGS framework that boosts both representation learning and cross-modal alignment during generation. We first revisit 2D feature selection for sparse-voxel-based 3D representation learning, propose Diffusion-Aligned Structured Latents (DA-SLAT) and couple it with a decoder-only architecture to improve 3DGS reconstruction fidelity. We also design a sparse-structure-aware diffusion framework, which integrates the Sparse-structure Multimodal Diffusion Transformer (SMDiT) and Modal-Aware Rotary Positional Embedding (MARoPE) to achieve geometry-agnostic 2D-3D alignment. Extensive benchmark experiments demonstrate that FLUX3D yields substantial improvements in appearance fidelity and significantly outperforms all state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods in generating high-quality 3DGS assets.

</details>


### [108] [Counting Trees from Satellite Imagery with Noisy Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24786)
*Dimitri Gominski,Maurice Mugabowindekwe,Qiue Xu,Xiaowei Tong,Martin Brandt,Hieu Le,Rasmus Fensholt,Dimitris Samaras,Loic Landrieu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将树木计数建模为空间密度匹配问题，通过非平衡最优传输（Unbalanced Optimal Transport）来监督，并引入基于传输残差的自校正机制以处理激光雷达噪声标注。在涵盖三大洲、三种卫星传感器、超过2.15亿棵树标注（含77.3万人工验证实例）的TinyTrees基准上，该方法优于检测、回归及基于传输的分布匹配基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星图像中的树木计数因以下挑战尚未被充分探索：（1）密集森林中树冠边界模糊，个体树木定义不明确；（2）大规模人工标注成本过高；（3）可扩展的机载激光雷达标注存在噪声且难以有效利用。

Method: 将树木计数建模为空间密度匹配问题，使用非平衡最优传输（Unbalanced Optimal Transport）作为监督信号，同时引入基于传输残差的自校正机制（self-correction mechanism），在训练过程中逐步优化噪声标注。

Result: 在包含三大洲、三种卫星传感器、超过2.15亿棵树标注（含77.3万人工验证实例）的TinyTrees基准上，该方法在树木计数任务上一致优于检测、回归及基于传输的分布匹配基线方法。

Conclusion: 非平衡传输与可靠性感知监督机制在大规模卫星图像树木计数任务中有效，证明了该方法在处理噪声标注和密集森林计数问题上的优势。

Abstract: Counting individual trees is a fundamental task for environmental monitoring, yet remains largely unexplored with satellite imagery. At these resolutions, isolated trees may still be identifiable, but crown boundaries become ambiguous in dense forests, making the notion of an individual tree inherently ill-defined. Moreover, large-scale manual annotations of individual trees are prohibitively expensive. While scalable supervision can be derived from airborne LiDAR, the resulting annotations are noisy and difficult to exploit effectively.
  We address these challenges by formulating tree counting as a spatial density matching problem supervised through Unbalanced Optimal Transport. This formulation naturally accommodates both precise localization of isolate trees and robust density estimation in dense forests. We further introduce a self-correction mechanism that leverages transport residuals to progressively refine noisy supervision during training.
  We evaluate our approach on TinyTrees, a new benchmark spanning three continents and three satellite sensors, comprising over 215 million tree annotations (including 773K manually verified instances) across 23,000 sq.km. Our method consistently outperforms detection-based, regression-based, and transport-based distribution-matching baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of unbalanced transport and reliability-aware supervision for large-scale tree counting from satellite imagery. Code, data and models are available at https://github.com/dgominski/treematch.

</details>


### [109] [Pocket-SLAM: Rendering-Area-Aware Pruning for Memory-Efficient 3DGS-SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24796)
*Leshu Li,Jie Peng,Yang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种渲染区域感知的剪枝策略，通过根据高斯点对有效渲染区域的贡献而非单纯依赖不透明度或梯度大小进行选择性移除，显著降低了3DGS-SLAM在自动驾驶等大尺度场景中的内存占用。在EuRoC和KITTI数据集上实现了超过60%的内存缩减和2倍以上的FPS提升，同时保持定位与建图精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS-SLAM在大尺度场景（如自动驾驶）中面临内存消耗随时间持续增长的严重限制，导致内存效率低下，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 提出渲染区域感知的剪枝策略，基于高斯点对有效渲染区域的贡献程度进行选择性移除，而非仅依赖不透明度或梯度大小等启发式指标，直接针对内存冗余的源头进行优化。

Result: 在EuRoC和KITTI数据集上，该方法在大尺度室外场景中优于现有剪枝方法，实现超过60%的内存缩减和2倍以上的FPS提升，同时保持定位与建图精度。

Conclusion: 渲染区域感知剪枝是推动3DGS-SLAM扩展至真实世界自动驾驶场景的有前景方向。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has garnered significant attention in Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) due to its advances in capturing fine-grained geometry features and synthesizing novel views. For SLAM in large-scale scenes, such as autonomous driving, 3DGS-SLAM faces a critical limitation: memory consumption increases continuously over time as Gaussian points accumulate, leading to poor memory efficiency and limiting its applicability. In this work, we propose a rendering-area-aware pruning strategy that selectively removes Gaussians based on their contribution to the effective rendering area, rather than solely relying on Gaussian-level heuristics such as opacity or gradient magnitude. This perspective directly targets the sources of memory redundancy, effectively reducing the peak memory footprint of 3DGS-SLAM during runtime. Evaluations on the EuRoC and KITTI datasets demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms existing pruning approaches in large-scale outdoor scenes, achieving over 60% memory reduction and more than 2 times FPS improvement while preserving localization and mapping accuracy. These results highlight rendering-area-aware pruning as a promising direction for scaling 3DGS-SLAM to real-world autonomous driving scenarios. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/UMN-ZhaoLab/Pocket-SLAM.git.

</details>


### [110] [DDStereo: Efficient Dual Decoder Transformers for Stereo 3D Road Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24805)
*Shiyi Mu,Zichong Gu,Zhiqi Ai,Yilin Gao,Shugong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DDStereo，一种双解码器立体Transformer，首次实现实时开放集立体3D目标检测，在速度上媲美单目方法，精度达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有立体3D检测方法虽精度优于单目，但推理速度慢，无法实时应用；且开放集/开放词汇检测在立体领域尚属空白。

Method: 采用双轻量级解码器分支：一个用于开放集前景2D检测，另一个用于3D属性回归，通过目标级查询共享实现统一对齐；设计紧凑的视差特征提取器和精简解码架构以提升推理效率。

Result: 在公开立体3D基准上，闭集和开放集协议下均达到SOTA精度，推理速度超越现有立体检测器，首次实现与单目方法相当的实时性能。

Conclusion: DDStereo填补了立体开放集3D检测的空白，同时解决了实时性问题，为立体3D检测的实际应用（如自动驾驶）提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Stereo-based 3D object detection still faces two critical safety challenges: real-time performance and open-set generalization. Existing stereo 3D methods typically achieve twice the accuracy of monocular methods but suffer from significantly lower inference speeds, making them unsuitable for real-time applications. Meanwhile, recent advances in open-world detection have introduced open-set and open-vocabulary algorithms in monocular 2D and 3D settings, yet stereo-based open-set detection remains largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose DDStereo, a novel Dual-Decoder Stereo Transformer for real-time open-set 3D object detection. DDStereo features two lightweight decoder branches: one for open-set foreground 2D detection and the other for 3D attribute regression. These decoders share object-level queries to achieve unified target-level alignment. To enhance inference efficiency, we designed a compact disparity feature extractor and a streamlined decoder architecture. Experiments on public stereo 3D benchmarks demonstrate that DDStereo achieves state-of-the-art accuracy under both closed-set and open-set protocols. Notably, our method surpasses existing stereo 3D detectors in inference speed and, for the first time, achieves real-time performance comparable to monocular approaches.

</details>


### [111] [GeoT2V-Bench: Benchmarking 3D Consistency in Text-to-Video Models via 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24829)
*Chenrui Fan,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 GeoT2V-Bench，一个基于3D重建的诊断基准，用于评估文本生成视频（T2V）模型在相机提示下是否能够支持刚体3D场景重建。通过从生成视频中估计相机参数、构建可变形和静态场景表示，并输出连续重建指标（包括图像运动、轨迹行为、渲染误差、流一致性和弹性与静态拟合的差距），对12个开源模型在80个场景上进行了3840次重建实验，发现不同指标常相互矛盾，揭示了生成视频作为静态场景采集时的互补性失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本生成视频模型虽能生成视觉上合理的相机运动视频，但缺乏评估其生成帧能否作为同一静态3D场景的多视角证据、支持显式刚体重建的能力，需要一种诊断性基准来量化这种几何一致性。

Method: 构建GeoT2V-Bench管线：使用VGGT估计每帧相机内参和位姿，拟合DeformableGS（可变形高斯泼溅），通过时间中位数聚合推导静态MedianGS代理，沿估计路径渲染；输出连续重建剖面，包含表观图像运动、估计轨迹行为、静态渲染误差、流一致性和弹性与静态拟合差距五项指标。

Result: 在12个开源模型、80个静态场景提示、4个随机种子共3840次重建实验中，发现可见运动、静态渲染误差、流一致性和弹性与静态拟合等指标常相互不一致，表明这些指标捕获了互补的失败模式。

Conclusion: GeoT2V-Bench能够有效地捕捉生成视频在作为全局静态场景采集时出现的互补性几何不一致失败模式，为评估T2V模型的3D几何一致性提供了系统化的诊断工具。

Abstract: Camera-prompted text-to-video (T2V) models are increasingly used to synthesize virtual camera captures, such as orbiting objects or moving through static scenes. For these outputs, visual plausibility is insufficient: the generated frames should also provide coherent multi-view evidence for a single static 3D scene. We introduce GeoT2V-Bench, a reconstruction-based diagnostic benchmark for evaluating whether camera-prompted T2V clips can support explicit rigid 3D reconstruction. Our pipeline estimates per-frame camera intrinsics and poses with VGGT-style geometry estimation, fits DeformableGS, derives a static MedianGS proxy by temporal-median aggregation, and renders this proxy along the estimated camera path. Instead of producing a pass/fail label or a single scalar score, GeoT2V-Bench reports a continuous reconstruction profile covering apparent image motion, estimated trajectory behavior, MedianGS static rendering error, static-render flow agreement, and the gap between flexible and static fits. On a fair-format four-seed evaluation with 3,840 completed reconstructions from 12 open-weight model configurations and 80 GeCo-Eval static-scene prompts, we find that visible motion, static rendering error, flow agreement, and flexible-vs-static behavior often disagree. GeoT2V-Bench therefore captures complementary failure modes that emerge when generated videos are tested as global static-scene acquisitions.

</details>


### [112] [Bridging the Manifold Gap: Riemannian Residual Line Search for One-Step Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24844)
*Hongzhu Yi,Zhongtian Luo,Tong Li,Yiyan Fan,Jungang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种Riemannian Residual Line Search方法，将一步扩散编辑中的保真度与编辑强度的矛盾转化为后验候选选择问题，通过在能量场传输上估计局部时间曲率构建更强编辑，再从源图像到该编辑形成残差路径，最终通过CLIP对齐选择最佳候选，在PIE-Bench++上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步扩散编辑器无需反演和迭代优化，速度快，但其单步传输更新需要在实现目标提示词与保留源图像之间取得平衡，而固定更新强度无法满足所有编辑类型的需求。

Method: 提出Riemannian Residual Line Search方法：(1) 通过估计提示词差异场的局部时间曲率构建更强编辑，将修正方向投影回原始一阶能量场传输估计的更新范数上；(2) 从源图像到该强编辑形成小残差路径；(3) 保留原始一阶输出作为一个候选，通过最大化目标提示词CLIP对齐度选择最终图像。

Result: 在包含700个样本、10种编辑类型的PIE-Bench++评估中，该方法在现有一步更新算法中达到了SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 该方法将保真度与编辑强度的矛盾重新定义为后验候选选择问题，而非设计新的编辑模型，从而在一步扩散编辑中实现了最优效果。

Abstract: One-step diffusion editors are fast because they avoid inversion and iterative optimization, but a single transport update must be aggressive enough to realize the target prompt and conservative enough to preserve the source image--and no fixed update strength satisfies both demands across edit types. We treat this tension as a post-hoc candidate-selection problem on top of energy-field transport rather than as a new editing model. Our proposed method, Riemannian Residual Line Search, first builds a stronger edit by estimating the local time curvature of the prompt-delta field and projecting the corrected direction back onto the update norm of the original first-order energy-field transport estimation. It then forms a small residual path from the source image to this strong edit, retains the original first-order output as one candidate, and picks the final image by maximizing target-prompt CLIP alignment. On a 700-sample PIE-Bench++ evaluation across 10 edit type IDs, our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance among current one-step update algorithms.

</details>


### [113] [Spherical-to-ERP Epipolar Rectification for Single-Axis Disparity in 360 Stereo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24847)
*Sahereh Obeidavi,Dieter Landes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文验证了将球面立体图像通过ERP投影预处理后，结合RAFT+EACS框架进行视差估计的有效性，在合成鱼眼数据集上实现了实时、准确且结构一致的视差图生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球面/鱼眼立体图像违背经典视差估计的几何假设（极线对应呈弯曲大圆路径，产生二维位移），需要验证传统管线能否直接扩展至360°成像场景。

Method: 采用球面到等距柱状投影（ERP）作为预处理步骤，将弯曲极线拉直恢复一维视差结构；基于RAFT密集光流+极线对齐通道选择（EACS）框架，仅保留基线对齐方向的光流分量作为视差。

Result: 在合成鱼眼立体数据集上，球面→ERP→RAFT+EACS管线能够生成准确、平滑且结构一致的视差图，且达到实时运行速度。

Conclusion: ERP预处理可与RAFT+EACS方法有效结合，为将传统立体视差估计管线扩展至360°成像提供了一条直接、实用且可解释的路径。

Abstract: Omnidirectional stereo images provide full-surround perception but violate the geometric assumptions of classical disparity estimation: in spherical or fisheye views, epipolar correspondences follow curved great-circle paths, producing two-dimensional displacements that cannot be treated as single-axis disparity before geometric rectification. In this work, we adopt a standard spherical-to-equirectangular (ERP) projection as a preprocessing step, which straightens epipolar curves and restores a one-dimensional disparity structure - horizontal for left-right rigs and vertical for top-bottom rigs. Building on our previously introduced RAFT + Epipolar-Aligned Channel Selection (EACS) framework, originally developed for rectilinear and ERP stereo, we examine whether the same modular pipeline remains accurate when the input originates from spherical stereo imagery. After ERP projection, dense optical flow from RAFT is reduced to disparity by retaining only the baseline-aligned flow component. Experiments on synthetic fisheye stereo datasets show that this spherical-to-ERP-to-RAFT+EACS pipeline produces accurate, smooth, and structurally consistent disparity maps at real-time speed. These findings confirm that established ERP preprocessing can be effectively combined with our earlier RAFT+EACS method to enable practical, interpretable, and efficient disparity estimation from spherical stereo, providing a straightforward pathway for extending conventional stereo pipelines to 360 imaging.

</details>


### [114] [FLAT: Feedforward Latent Triangle Splatting for Geometrically Accurate Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24876)
*Orest Kupyn,Goutam Bhat,Philipp Henzler,Fabian Manhardt,Christian Rupprecht,Federico Tombari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLAT是一种从单张图像通过视频扩散潜变量直接解码三角形图元（triangle splats）以生成可探索3D场景的新方法，相比解码3D高斯点云，它实现了更好的几何精度并支持游戏引擎实时渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的前馈潜变量场景解码器通常输出体积化的3D高斯点云，缺乏明确定义的表面，限制了其在仿真或标准图形学管线中的应用。因此需要解码表面对齐的图元，使其既可渲染又更接近显式几何资产。

Method: 提出FLAT方法，包含两个关键组件：1）基于射线的旋转参数化（ray-centered rotation parameterization）用于三角形回归；2）新颖的乘积窗函数（product window function）改善可微三角形渲染中的梯度流动。从视频扩散潜变量中直接解码三角形图元。

Result: 在标准基准测试上，FLAT相比最先进的前馈基线方法实现了显著更好的几何精度，同时保持了有竞争力的视觉质量。轻量级的测试时精化步骤可将预测的三角形网格转换为支持实时渲染的全不透明游戏引擎表示。

Conclusion: 首次证明了三角形图元可以从视频扩散潜变量中直接解码，并在相同训练设置下对3DGS、2DGS和三角形图元变体进行了系统性分析，揭示了场景生成中不同表示方法的权衡。

Abstract: Generating explorable 3D scenes from a single image requires strong generative priors and accurate geometric representations suitable for downstream use. Current video diffusion models offer high-quality generation and implicitly encode multi-view geometric structure in latent space. However, existing feedforward latent scene decoders typically output volumetric 3D Gaussians that lack a well-defined surface, limiting their use in simulation or standard graphics pipelines. This motivates decoding surface-aligned primitives that are not only renderable but also closer to explicit geometric assets. We ask whether compressed video diffusion latents can be mapped directly to explicit surface primitives in a single pass. To this end, we introduce FLAT and, for the first time, show that triangle splats can be decoded directly from video diffusion latents. Compared with decoding 3D Gaussians, predicting flat primitives is notoriously more challenging due to high sensitivity to primitive orientations, oftentimes leading to poor gradient flow. FLAT solves with two key ingredients: a ray-centered rotation parameterization for triangle regression and a novel product window function that improves gradient flow during differentiable triangle rendering. On standard benchmarks, FLAT achieves significantly better geometric accuracy while maintaining competitive visual quality compared to state-of-the-art feedforward baselines. We further show that a lightweight test-time refinement step converts the predicted triangle soup into a fully opaque, game-engine-ready representation that supports real-time rendering. By evaluating 3DGS, 2DGS, and triangle splatting variants under an identical training setup, we provide the first systematic analysis of representation tradeoffs in feedforward scene generation. The project page is available at https://flat-splat.github.io

</details>


### [115] [BenchX: Benchmarking AI Models for Cancer Detection and Localization with Demographic and Protocol Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24883)
*Qi Chen,Wenxuan Li,Pedro R. A. S. Bassi,Xinze Zhou,Jakob Wasserthal,Ibrahim Ethem Hamamci,Sezgin Er,Ashwin Kumar,Yiwen Ye,Yuhan Wang,Yuyin Zhou,Akshay S. Chaudhari,Curtis Langlotz,Kang Wang,Yang Yang,Alan L. Yuille,Zongwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含85,355次CT扫描的大规模开放基准，系统评估了12个肿瘤检测AI模型在不同肿瘤大小、位置、患者亚组和成像协议下的表现，发现当前最优模型在罕见或代表性不足的亚组（如年轻、女性、非裔美国人）中表现较差，并利用大语言模型实现可扩展且可复现的亚组信息提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型在医学影像中表现不一致，尤其在患者人口统计学和成像协议变化时（如检测小肿瘤、不同对比剂相位、不同年龄或性别患者），现有评估缺乏对亚组层面系统性的量化分析。

Method: 构建一个包含85,355次CT扫描的大规模开放基准，系统评估12个肿瘤检测AI模型；利用大语言模型（LLMs）从临床数据中提取和组织亚组信息，实现可扩展且可复现的分析。

Result: 当前最先进的AI模型在平均准确率上表现优异，但在罕见或代表性不足的亚组（如年轻、女性、非裔美国人）中性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 该基准为构建更可靠、更鲁棒的肿瘤检测AI模型奠定了基础，并强调了在医学影像和计算机视觉中进行严格的亚组层面评估的必要性。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) has achieved remarkable success in medical imaging, but it is widely recognized that these models often perform inconsistently across real-world clinical settings. Such inconsistencies occur when patient demographics and imaging protocols vary, for example, in detecting small tumors, analyzing scans from different contrast phases, or evaluating patients of different ages or sexes. To quantify these inconsistencies, we develop a large-scale, open benchmark of 85,355 CT scans that systematically evaluates 12 tumor-detection AI models across tumor size, location, patient subgroup, and imaging protocol. We leverage large language models (LLMs) to extract and organize subgroup information from clinical data, which makes the analysis both scalable and reproducible. Our benchmark reveals that current state-of-the-art AI models, optimized for average accuracy, perform poorly in rare or underrepresented subgroups, such as young, female African Americans. However, collecting sufficient annotated data for these rare cases is often impractical. The benchmark provides a foundation for building more reliable and robust AI models for tumor detection and highlighting the need for rigorous, subgroup-level evaluation in medical imaging and computer vision. Datasets, code

</details>


### [116] [DiffusionBench: On Holistic Evaluation of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24888)
*Xingjian Leng,Jaskirat Singh,Zhanhao Liang,Ethan Smith,Martin Bell,Aninda Saha,Yuhui Yuan,Liang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NanoGen统一框架，证明ImageNet类条件生成与文生图任务上的DiT方法排名无相关性（Pearson相关系数-0.377至-0.580），建议用DiffusionBench替代仅用ImageNet评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前DiT研究仅以ImageNet类条件生成评估，这无法反映生成建模的真正进展；文生图虽被认为是更好的替代但因成本高被忽视，作者认为这一认知不再成立。

Method: 提出NanoGen统一训练与评估框架，支持RAE、VAE、像素空间和MeanFlow扩散方法，仅需12行配置变更即可从ImageNet切换至文生图；训练21个潜扩散模型后计算ImageNet与文生图间的指标排名相关性。

Result: ImageNet与文生图的方法排名无强相关性（Pearson相关系数-0.377至-0.580）；NanoGen在ImageNet上匹配SOTA基线，同时以相当算力训练出有竞争力的文生图模型。

Conclusion: DiT研究必须在ImageNet和文生图两项任务上共同评估，提出DiffusionBench作为替代单一ImageNet评估的综合性基准。

Abstract: Diffusion transformer (DiT) research on image generation has converged to a single evaluation setup: class-conditional generation on ImageNet. While methods improve the FID and related metrics, it is increasingly unclear whether they reflect real progress in generative modeling. The natural alternative, i.e., text-to-image (T2I) generation, is perceived as too costly or inconvenient to train and evaluate and is often skipped. We argue that this perception no longer holds. We introduce NanoGen, a unified DiT training and evaluation framework. NanoGen matches state-of-the-art DiT baselines on ImageNet and, with 12 lines of configuration change, also trains competitive text-to-image models. It currently supports RAE, VAE, pixel-space, and MeanFlow diffusion methods under both ImageNet and T2I setups. Under NanoGen, training T2I requires comparable compute to ImageNet. After training 21 latent diffusion models with NanoGen, we observe that method ranking shows no strong correlation between ImageNet and T2I generation: Pearson correlation is between -0.377 and -0.580 across three metrics. This suggests that a method which improves class-conditional ImageNet FID may show no corresponding improvement on T2I, clearly indicating the necessity of evaluating DiTs on both tasks. To this end, we summarize ImageNet and text-to-image results, which yields DiffusionBench, a holistic benchmark for DiT research. We recommend reporting DiffusionBench in place of ImageNet alone: methods that improve DiffusionBench are more likely to reflect broader progress.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [SpaCE: Rethinking Spatial Capacity and Generalization in Multi-Frame Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23717)
*Mariana Costa,Camila Ferreira,Alberlucia Rafael Soarez,Alejandro Torres*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpaCE理论框架，首次从信息论、样本复杂度、PAC-Bayes泛化边界和偏差-方差权衡四个角度，系统刻画了多模态大语言模型在多帧输入下的空间推理能力，并在多个基准上验证了理论界紧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在多帧空间推理方面取得了显著经验进展，但其理论基础完全空白，缺乏对空间推理能力、样本效率和泛化保证的理论理解。

Method: 构建SpaCE理论框架，从信息论上界、样本复杂度（$Θ(d_{\mathrm{eff}} \cdot K_{\max} / (\varepsilon^2 \cdot δ))$）、PAC-Bayes分布偏移泛化界、显式3D表示与隐式推理的偏差-方差权衡四个维度进行理论分析，并在MultiSPA、CA-VQA、SpatialRGPT基准上验证。

Result: 推导了空间推理准确率的信息论上界；给出了样本复杂度界；提供了分布偏移下的PAC-Bayes泛化界；刻画了显式3D与隐式推理方法之间的偏差-方差权衡及交叉条件；实证表明理论界紧且帧互补性是多帧空间推理能力的关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: SpaCE为理解多模态大语言模型在多帧空间推理中何时、为何以及如何成功提供了首个原则性的理论基础。

Abstract: Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable empirical progress in spatial understanding through large-scale training on spatial visual question answering datasets. However, the theoretical foundations of multi-frame spatial reasoning remain entirely unexplored. We present SpaCE, a rigorous theoretical framework that characterizes the spatial reasoning capacity, sample complexity, and generalization guarantees of MLLMs operating on multi-frame inputs. We establish four main results. First, we prove an information-theoretic upper bound on spatial reasoning accuracy in terms of the mutual information between multi-frame observations and spatial targets. Second, we derive a sample complexity bound of order $Θ(d_{\mathrm{eff}} \cdot K_{\max} / (\varepsilon^2 \cdot δ))$, where $d_{\mathrm{eff}}$ is the effective spatial dimension and $K_{\max}$ bounds the KL divergence of the learned posterior. Third, we provide a PAC-Bayes generalization bound for multi-frame spatial reasoning under distribution shift. Fourth, we formally characterize the bias-variance trade-off between explicit 3D representations and implicit reasoning approaches, identifying the crossover conditions under which each paradigm is provably preferable. We validate our theoretical predictions on the MultiSPA, CA-VQA, and SpatialRGPT benchmarks, demonstrating that our bounds are empirically tight and that frame complementarity is the key driver of multi-frame spatial capacity. Our framework provides the first principled theoretical foundation for understanding when, why, and how multi-frame spatial reasoning in MLLs succeeds.

</details>


### [118] [An Empirical Study of Entropy-Conserving Binarization in H.264/AVC CABAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23753)
*Vinamra Singh*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文在CABAC框架下将熵守恒二值化（ECB）与UEG、单上下文Huffman和按位位置分配上下文的Huffman变体（HuffmanPos）进行了系统性对比实验。结果表明，在低熵源上，码率差距主要由上下文分配机制驱动，而非二值化的符号平均bin数；ECB在大字母表上面临7-10倍的解码延迟惩罚，作者提出了交错单遍变体以弥补这一缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECB理论上能无损保持从m元数据到m-1个二进制串的熵映射，但从未在真实生产级二进制算术编码器中评估过，缺乏与现有主流二值化方案（如UEG、Huffman）的实际性能对比。

Method: 将ECB集成到从零实现的CABAC中，与UEG、单上下文Huffman、HuffmanPos共享同一个M-coder后端；在合成源、程序化图像的DCT残差以及Kodak 24图完整套件（2480次往返测试，逐位验证）上进行基准测试。

Result: 程序化图像上Q=8时ECB超越单上下文Huffman，Q=32时领先27个百分点；Kodak上ECB在所有Q值下均优于单上下文Huffman，差距从0.031到0.113 bits/symbol；HuffmanPos在15个测试源单元中12个优于ECB，其余3个最多落后0.56个百分点。ECB解码延迟在大字母表上比UEG高7-10倍。

Conclusion: 在低熵条件下，码率差距的主要驱动因素是bin流上的上下文分配策略而非二值化本身产生的每符号bin数；ECB的解码效率瓶颈源于O(N*m)解码器，可通过交错单遍变体解决。

Abstract: CABAC, the entropy coder of H.264/AVC and the basis for HEVC and VVC, decomposes multi-symbol values into bins via a binarization scheme before a binary arithmetic coder. H.264 uses Truncated Unary plus k-th order Exp-Golomb (UEG); alternatives include canonical Huffman and the entropy-conserving binarization (ECB), which provably preserves entropy mapping m-ary data to m-1 binary strings but has not been evaluated inside a production binary arithmetic coder. We integrate ECB into a from-scratch CABAC implementation alongside UEG, single-context Huffman, and a Huffman variant with per-bin-position contexts (HuffmanPos), all sharing one M-coder backend. We benchmark all four on synthetic sources, DCT residuals from a procedural image, and the full 24-image Kodak suite (2,480 round-trip trials, bit-exact verified). On the procedural image, a sparsity-driven crossover at Q=8 lets ECB overtake single-context Huffman, reaching 27 percentage points below at Q=32. On Kodak the crossover shifts below the tested range and ECB beats single-context Huffman at every Q, the gap growing from 0.031 to 0.113 bits per symbol. HuffmanPos, sharing Huffman's codewords but allocating one context per bin position, beats ECB on 12 of 15 source cells and loses by at most 0.56 percentage points on the other three, despite the same per-symbol bin count as single-context Huffman. This isolates the dominant mechanism: at low source entropy the rate gap is driven primarily by context allocation over the bin stream, not the binarization's per-symbol bin count. ECB's rate efficiency costs 7 to 10x in decoder latency on large alphabets, traced to an O(N*m) decoder; we sketch an interleaved single-pass variant that would close this gap. Code, benchmarks, and raw data are open source.

</details>


### [119] [Promise and challenges of heart chamber segmentation from non-contrast CT scans using contrastive unpaired image translation: a feasibility study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23879)
*Jing Wang,Tong Yu,Hao-En Lu,Zixue Zeng,Joseph K. Leader,Xin Meng,Jianbing Zhu,Jiantao Pu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出ChameleonNet框架，利用对比非配对图像翻译（CUT+DCL损失）将增强CT转化为非增强CT，结合Hausdorff距离损失增强的nnU-Net实现非增强CT心脏四腔室分割。在合成非增强图像上DSC达0.91-0.94，HD95为3.63-5.74mm；在真实非增强CT上体积Pearson相关系数0.82-0.93，MAPE 9.22%-20.79%，但左室和右室体积误差仍需改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现非增强CT心脏四腔室分割，避免在非增强CT上需要大量人工标注，利用增强CT已有的标注数据通过图像翻译间接实现分割。

Method: (1) 使用CUT网络结合DCL损失将增强CT翻译为非增强CT；(2) 在合成非增强图像上训练Hausdorff距离损失增强的nnU-Net分割模型，标注来自增强CT的四腔室（左心房、左心室、右心房、右心室）。

Result: 合成非增强图像：DSC 0.91-0.94，HD95 3.63-5.74mm。真实非增强CT体积一致性：Pearson r 0.82-0.93（p<0.001），MAPE 9.22%-20.79%，MPE -12.52%至4.67%。

Conclusion: ChameleonNet证明了无需人工非增强标注即可实现非增强CT心脏分割的可行性，但LV和RV体积误差较大，临床使用前需进一步优化和验证。

Abstract: Purpose: To evaluate the feasibility and challenges of heart chamber segmentation from non-contrast CT scans using contrastive unpaired image translation and deep learning-based segmentation. Approach: We developed ChameleonNet, a framework utilizing the Contrastive Unpaired Translation (CUT) network with decoupled contrastive learning (DCL) loss to synthesize non-contrast CT from contrast CT scans. Using annotations of four heart chambers (left atrium (LA), left ventricle (LV), right atrium (RA), and right ventricle (RV)) from contrast scans, we trained a Hausdorff distance loss-enhanced nnU-Net on synthesized non-contrast images. The translation model was trained with 35,538 contrast-enhanced and 37,197 non-contrast CT slices. The segmentation model was trained with 292 synthesized non-contrast scans. Performance was evaluated using Dice similarity coefficient (DSC) and 95th Hausdorff distance (HD95) on 36 synthesized non-contrast scans, and volume agreement on 36 real non-contrast CT scans was assessed using Pearson correlation, mean absolute percentage error (MAPE), and mean percentage error (MPE). Results: The segmentation model achieved DSC of 0.94 (0.01), 0.91 (0.04), 0.92 (0.03), 0.93 (0.02), and HD95 of 3.63 (1.49), 5.74 (4.08), 5.18 (1.77), 5.51 (3.21) mm on synthesized non-contrast images for LA, LV, RA, and RV, respectively. On real non-contrast CT scans, Pearson correlations were 0.93, 0.82, 0.87, and 0.89 (all p<0.001), with MAPE ranging from 9.22% to 20.79%, and MPE ranging from -12.52% to 4.67%. Conclusions: ChameleonNet demonstrated feasibility for heart chamber segmentation from non-contrast CT without manual non-contrast annotations. However, volume errors, particularly for LV and RV, indicate that further refinement and validation are needed before clinical use.

</details>


### [120] [E-MRL: Cross-view Aligned Evidence-driven Multimodal Reinforcement Learning for Reliable 3D Tumor Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23888)
*Sijing Li,Zhongwei Qiu,Zhuoya Wang,Boxiang Yun,Zhenyu Yi,Jianwei Xu,Wenqiao Zhang,Yingda Xia,Ling Zhang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出E-MRL，一种跨视图对齐的基于证据的多模态强化学习框架，将3D CT报告生成建模为"诊断-定位-验证"的马尔可夫决策过程，通过引入关键证据切片选择和跨视图一致性奖励，显著减少视觉幻觉并提升诊断准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在3D医学报告生成中常出现视觉幻觉和缺乏CT数据 grounding 的问题，SFT和RL方法仅优化文本保真度，奖励的是基于语言先验的正确诊断而非真实视觉感知。

Method: 将报告生成建模为"诊断-定位-验证"的MDP；显式训练模型选择关键证据切片并生成全局诊断报告；提出跨视图一致性奖励，验证金标准报告与所选关键切片的局部视觉重查询之间的语义对齐。

Result: 在大规模3D CT肿瘤数据集上，E-MRL相比SFT和RL基线显著减少幻觉、提升诊断准确性。

Conclusion: E-MRL提供了一种临床可解释的、基于视觉证据的3D CT肿瘤分析解决方案。

Abstract: While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) show great promise in volumetric medical report generation, they frequently suffer from visual hallucinations and a lack of grounding in 3D CT data. Current Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning (RL) strategies typically optimize text fidelity alone, essentially rewarding correct diagnoses derived from language priors rather than genuine visual perception. To address this, we propose cross-view aligned Evidence-driven Multimodal Reinforcement Learning (Evidence-MRL, noted as E-MRL), a reliable RL reasoning framework that formulates the generation process as a Markov Decision Process of "diagnosis-localization-verification". Unlike standard approaches, our model is explicitly trained to identify a "key evidence slice" alongside the global diagnostic report, grounding its findings in verifiable visual evidence. Crucially, we introduce a novel cross-view consistency reward, which validates the semantic alignment between the golden-standard report and a local visual re-query of the selected key slice, providing additional rewards for correctly-localized reasoning. Experiments on large-scale 3D CT tumor datasets demonstrate that E-MRL significantly reduces hallucinations and improves diagnostic accuracy compared to SFT and RL baselines, offering a clinically interpretable solution for visually-grounded and tumor analysis.

</details>


### [121] [A Dual Edge Spatial Jacobian Image Graph for Interpretable Diabetic Retinopathy Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24168)
*Inam Ullah,Imran Razzak,Shoaib Jameel*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种双边缘空间-雅可比图像图方法，用于可解释的糖尿病视网膜病变分级，通过融合血管-病灶几何关系与嵌入-生物标志物敏感性，实现可解释的图像级分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DR分级方法仅提供图像级标签，缺乏对病变证据在视网膜血管周围分布的理解及其与定量血管生物标志物之间关系的解释能力。

Method: 将每张眼底图像构建为图节点，包含四个对齐的证据流（AutoMorph血管信息、DR-XAI病变证据图、病变对比嵌入、AutoMorph形态学生物标志物），通过空间边缘分支编码血管-病灶几何关系，通过雅可比分支建模嵌入-生物标志物敏感性，最后使用轻量双令牌注意力融合两类边缘。

Result: 在2,910张APTOS图像上，完整图达到0.8076准确率、0.8312二次加权kappa、0.5915宏F1、0.9330相邻等级准确率；可转诊DR达到0.9055准确率和0.9711 AUROC。

Conclusion: 该框架定位为可解释的表征学习工具，用于病变-生物标志物假设生成，而非可直接部署的临床分类器。

Abstract: Automated diabetic retinopathy (DR) grading from colour fundus photographs can achieve strong predictive performance, but clinical interpretation requires more than an image-level label. It requires understanding how lesion evidence is distributed around retinal vessels and how this evidence relates to quantitative vascular biomarkers. We present a dual-edge spatial-Jacobian image graph for interpretable DR grading. Each fundus image is represented as a graph node with four aligned evidence streams: AutoMorph vessel information ($X_1$), DR-XAI-style lesion evidence maps ($X_2$), a 128-dimensional lesion-based contrastive image embedding ($X_3$), and AutoMorph morphometric biomarkers ($X_4$). The spatial edge branch ($X_{12}$) encodes vessel-lesion geometry, while the Jacobian branch ($X_{34}$) models embedding-biomarker sensitivity. Lightweight two-token attention fuses both edge families into a final image graph. On 2,910 matched non-augmented APTOS images, the full graph achieves 0.8076 accuracy, 0.8312 quadratic weighted kappa, 0.5915 macro-F1, and 0.9330 adjacent-grade accuracy; referable DR reaches 0.9055 accuracy and 0.9711 AUROC. The framework is positioned as an explainable representation-learning tool for lesion-biomarker hypothesis generation, rather than as a deployment-ready clinical classifier. The code is available at https://github.com/Inamullah-Colab/dual-edge-dr-graph-xai.

</details>


### [122] [High Resolution Sediment-Specific Surface Soil Moisture Retrieval Using Sentinel-1 Time Series and Auxiliary Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24364)
*Alireza Hamedianfar,Oleg Antropov,Matthieu Molinier,Ulla Salmela,Hanna Kukkula,Lauri Seitsonen,Pauliina Liwata-Kenttälä,Maarit Middleton*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本研究利用原位土壤湿度传感器与多传感器SAR影像结合，通过机器学习方法（XGBoost、LightGBM、RF等）对芬兰东南部石灰岩矿区的表层土壤水分进行反演。最佳配置下RMSE降至0.037-0.050 m³/m³，R²达0.90。基于树的集成方法表现最优，加入沉积物信息可提升仅用Sentinel-1数据的反演精度超过2 vol-%，但在多源特征集下增益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有土壤水分反演方法在矿区复杂地质条件下的精度有限，缺乏针对特定沉积物类型校准的模型，且多源遥感数据融合策略有待优化。

Method: 结合原位电容传感器与Sentinel-1 C-band SAR、Sentinel-2光学数据、地形及温度预测因子，采用XGBoost、LightGBM、随机森林、线性回归和K近邻回归五种方法，构建沉积物类型特异性模型进行土壤水分反演。

Result: 最优配置（综合Sentinel-1后向散射、时序土壤湿度指数、Sentinel-2光学、地形和温度预测因子）下，RMSE为0.037-0.050 m³/m³，R²达0.90。树集成方法（LightGBM、RF、XGBoost）表现最稳定准确。粘土和有机质土壤误差最低，浮选砂和砾石误差最高。加入沉积物信息使Sentinel-1-only反演精度提升超2 vol-%，但对多源特征集增益有限。

Conclusion: 沉积物特异性校准模型可显著提升矿区土壤水分反演精度，尤其在仅使用SAR数据时。但多源特征融合可部分替代沉积物信息的作用，未来研究可聚焦于更精细的沉积物分类与模型泛化能力。

Abstract: In this study, we examine the potential of continuous ground moisture monitoring over a mining site using a combination of in-situ soil moisture sensors and multi-sensor SAR images. We focus on assessing and improving methodologies for retrieval of surface soil moisture, i.e. ground moisture, from SAR measurements focusing on detailed in situ reference observations for several key geomaterials, i.e. sediments, typical in the study site. The mining site represents a limestone quarry locate in the southeastern Finland. Our hypothesis is that sediment-specific well-calibrated models can be instrumental in improving soil moisture retrieval under different weather conditions to produce spatially explicit soil moisture estimates at high resolution compared to baseline approaches. Studied SAR data are represented by Copernicus Sentinel-1 C-band images, while auxiliary datasets include optical Sentinel-2 data. Reference data were collected using IoT enabled capacitance sensors. The examined machine learning methods include Xgboost, LightGBM, RFs, linear regression and k-nearest neighbors regression. The best performance was achieved with the most comprehensive feature set which combines Sentinel-1 backscatter, time-series based soil moisture indices, Sentinel-2 optical, topographic, and temperature predictors. In the best sediment-area-level configurations, RMSE decreased to 0.037-0.050 m^3 m^(-3) (3.7-5.0 volumetric % points), with R^2 values reaching 0.90. Tree-based ensemble methods, especially LightGBM, RF, and XGBoost, provided the most accurate and stable predictions. Accuracy varied by sediment texture, with the lowest errors for clay and organic soil and higher errors for flotation sand and gravel. Adding sediment information improved Sentinel-1-only retrievals by more than 2 vol-%, but provided little additional benefit when richer multi-source feature sets were used.

</details>


### [123] [Female-RHINO: A Real-Time Scanner-Integrated Framework for Automated Quantitative Uterine MRI Analysis and Structured Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24390)
*Deepak Bhatia,Saad Ahmad,Smiti Tripathy,Maria Camila Bustos Vivas,Lieselotte Kratzsch,Anika Knupfer,Jordina Aviles Verdera,Susanne Schulz-Heise,Matthias May,Jana Hutter*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出Female-RHINO，一个与MRI扫描仪集成的实时AI框架，可在图像采集过程中自动完成子宫MRI定量分析和结构化报告输出，无需人工交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子宫MRI标准化评估因解剖变异、观察者依赖和缺乏工作流集成的自动化分析工具而面临挑战。

Method: 端到端系统，集成与MRI扫描仪的在线通信和基于深度学习的分析，包括分割模型和 landmarks 检测模型，在500+多中心数据集上训练，完成子宫体积测量、常见偶发征检测（肌瘤、纳博特囊肿）及六个解剖 landmarks 提取。

Result: 子宫Dice 0.82，肌瘤Dice 0.80，landmark检测径向误差3.7mm，端到端处理时间<70秒，前瞻性部署中具有良好观察者间一致性。

Conclusion: 该系统实现了实时扫描仪集成AI的子宫MRI自动分析与报告，有望提升盆腔影像的标准化、效率和临床工作流程。

Abstract: Standardized assessment of uterine MRI remains challenging due to anatomical variability, observer dependence, and the lack of workflow-integrated automated analysis tools. This work presents Female-RHINO: (R)eproductive (H)ealth (I)maging A(N)alysis T(O)ol, a real-time AI-assisted framework for automated quantitative uterine MRI analysis and structured reporting during image acquisition. We present an end-to-end system that integrates inline communication with the MRI scanner and deep learning-based analysis to derive quantitative uterine biomarkers from sagittal T2-weighted pelvic MRI. The framework combines segmentation and anatomical landmark detection models trained and evaluated on more than 500 multi-center datasets spanning diverse protocols, vendors, and patient populations. It performs volumetry, detects and quantifies common incidental findings such as fibroids and Nabothian cysts, and extracts six anatomical landmarks for biometric assessment. Results are compiled into a structured clinician-oriented report with integrated visualizations, without manual interaction. Evaluation on independent retrospective and prospective cohorts demonstrated robust performance across varying acquisition settings. Mean Dice similarity coefficients were 0.82 for the uterus and 0.80 for fibroids, with lower but consistent agreement for Nabothian cysts. Landmark detection achieved a mean radial error of 3.7 mm. End-to-end processing was completed in under 70 seconds, enabling availability of results during the ongoing scan. Prospective deployment yielded immediate, standardized, and reproducible analyses supported by inter-observer agreement. The proposed system enables real-time scanner-integrated AI for automated uterine MRI analysis and reporting, with potential to improve standardization, efficiency, and clinical workflow in pelvic imaging.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Verifiable Foundation Models for Robot Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23754)
*Davide Corsi,Kyungmin Kim,Roy Fox*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FEARL框架通过将机器人策略分解为大型控制器（处理高维感知和任务推理）和小型安全模块（处理低维安全传感器观测和形式化验证），在保持基础模型表达能力的同时实现了可验证的安全性。实验在三个模拟域和物理机器人上验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署基础模型进行机器人控制面临核心矛盾：模型的强表达能力使其不透明且难以形式化分析，现有的验证工具无法处理这类模型。

Method: 提出FEARL框架，采用模块化架构分解策略：大型Controller处理高维感知和任务推理，小型Safety模块接收低维安全传感器观测和Controller的上下文嵌入，输出最终动作。Safety模块可进行形式化验证。

Result: 在三个模拟机器人域上使用多种Controller骨干网络（包括预训练视觉-语言-动作模型）验证了分解策略的任务求解能力；并将学习策略从模拟环境迁移到物理机器人。

Conclusion: FEARL通过架构分解使得形式化验证变得可行，同时保留了控制器的表达能力；低维安全接口支持实用的仿真到现实迁移。

Abstract: Deploying foundation models for robot control raises a central challenge: the expressive power that enables rich, multimodal perception also makes these models opaque and difficult to analyze formally, rendering them intractable for existing verification tools. In this paper, we present FEARL (Foundation-Enabled Assured Robot Learning), a framework that addresses this tension through a modular architectural decomposition. FEARL separates the policy into a large Controller (C) responsible for high-dimensional perception and task reasoning, and a small Safety module (S) that receives low-dimensional observations from dedicated safety sensors together with a bounded context embedding from C and produces the final action. Since many robot safety requirements, such as collision avoidance and workspace boundary constraints, can be expressed over these safety sensor observations, formal verification can be applied to S rather than to the full foundation-model backbone. This makes formal analysis tractable with existing tools while preserving the Controller's expressive power for task reasoning. To show that the decomposed policy remains capable of solving diverse tasks, we evaluate FEARL on three simulated robotic domains using multiple Controller backbones and training procedures, including pretrained off-the-shelf vision-language-action models. We further transfer the learned policy from one of our simulated tasks to a physical robot, suggesting that the low-dimensional safety interface supports practical sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [125] [Engineering Reliable Autonomous Systems: Challenges and Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23760)
*Marie Farrell,Matt Luckcuck,Angelo Ferrando,Rafael C. Cardoso,Natasha Alechina,Marco Autili,Diana Benjumea Hernandez,Luciana Brasil Rebelo dos Santos,Daniela Briola,Ana Cavalcanti,Christian Colombo,Louise A. Dennis,Clare Dixon,Michael Fisher,Mario Gleirscher,Taylor Johnson,Charles Lesire,Livia Lestingi,Sven Linker,Brian Logan,Colin Paterson,Fabio Papacchini,Patrizio Pelliccione,Pedro Ribeiro,Maike Schwammberger,Silvia Lizeth Tapia Tarifa,Hazel Taylor,Jim Woodcock,Mengwei Xu,Yi Yang,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文报道了2024年洛伦兹中心"工程可靠自主系统"研讨会（ERAS）的讨论成果，汇集了形式化方法和自主系统社区的研究人员与行业从业者，围绕自主系统的验证与确认技术、工程实践和软件架构三大主题，形成了当前挑战及解决路径的路线图，旨在支持未来研究与工业合作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主系统日益普及，亟需简便易用的可靠性工程技术。该研讨会旨在连接学术界（形式化方法社区与自主系统工程社区）与工业界，识别关键挑战并规划解决路径。

Method: 通过组织跨社区研讨会（FMAS和AREA社区共同组织），邀请学术界研究人员、工业从业者及面临独特工程挑战的行业代表，围绕三个主题进行讨论：自主系统的验证与确认技术、实际自主系统工程、安全自主系统的软件架构。

Result: 产出成果包括：自主系统可靠性领域的挑战目录，以及相应的解决路径路线图。部分挑战可通过已有学术成果解决但尚未在工业实践中普及；其余挑战仍需进一步研究。

Conclusion: 该路线图旨在支持未来研究与工业合作，推动自主系统可靠性工程从学术成果向工业实践的转化，并指引未解决问题的研究方向。

Abstract: Engineering reliable autonomous systems is an important and growing topic in computer science. As autonomous systems become more prevalent, easy-to-use techniques for building them reliably are increasingly important.
  This workshop report captures and expands on the discussions at the Lorentz Center Workshop "Engineering Reliable Autonomous Systems" (ERAS), held from 10 to 14 June 2024. The workshop was co-organised by the organisers of the Workshop on Formal Methods for Autonomous Systems (FMAS) and the Workshop on Agents and Robots for reliable Engineered Autonomy (AREA). It brought together members of the FMAS and AREA communities, industry practitioners, and representatives from sectors where autonomous systems pose distinctive engineering challenges.
  The workshop focused on three main research topics: techniques for verification and validation of autonomous systems; engineering real-world autonomous systems; and software architectures for safe autonomous systems. Its main outcome is a catalogue of challenges in these areas and, most importantly, a pathway to solutions. Some challenges can already be tackled by techniques that are well known in academia but have not yet become regularly used in practice. Other challenges remain unresolved and require further research. This roadmap is intended to support future research and industrial collaboration.

</details>


### [126] [Enforcing Human-like Kinematics in Dexterous Piano Playing via Adversarial Posture Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23848)
*Bin Qiu,Yanming Shao,Guanyu Cai,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出对抗性姿势正则化(APR)方法，利用少量非结构化人类弹琴数据，通过对抗性目标匹配策略姿势分布与人类先验，使高自由度双手在物理仿真弹钢琴时姿势更自然，在三种类人指标上显著优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习训练的双手机器人手在物理仿真中弹钢琴时，仅靠任务奖励或逆运动学(IK)会导致不自然的姿势和关节过度伸展，而获取歌曲对齐的专家示范数据成本高昂。

Method: 提出Adversarial Posture Regularization (APR)，使用消费级Meta Quest 3采集少量非结构化人类弹琴手部运动数据，重定向至Shadow Hand，通过对抗性目标使策略的姿势分布匹配人类先验。

Result: 在cPSI、BSE和FAC三种类人指标以及视觉质量上均显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: APR能有效利用少量非对齐的人类数据正则化强化学习策略，生成更自然类人的手部姿势，无需昂贵专家示范。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning can train bimanual dexterous hands to play piano in physics simulation with high note accuracy, but for high-DoF dexterous hands, relying solely on task rewards or IK inversion often leads to unnatural postures and joint overextension. We propose \textit{Adversarial Posture Regularization (APR)}. It avoids expensive, song-aligned expert demonstration data and instead uses a small amount of casual human playing data. By matching the distribution of the posture of the policy with the human prior through an adversarial objective, APR encourages more human-like hand shapes. Meanwhile, we collect and release unstructured hand motion data of piano playing using a consumer-grade Meta Quest 3, and retarget the key motion information to the Shadow Hand. Finally, we achieve significantly better performance than prior methods on all three human-likeness metrics (cPSI, BSE, and FAC) as well as in visual quality. Project repository: https://github.com/APRProject/APRPianist.

</details>


### [127] [Topological Online Learning for Displacement-based Formation Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23901)
*Saksham Sharma,Shubhankar Gupta,Sumant A Gunagi,Suresh Sundaram*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TOLD框架，一种实时边级自适应方法，通过在线更新交互拓扑权重来最小化编队形变。包含OGF（无约束权重）和OExpGF（非负凸权重）两种策略。理论证明OExpGF保证渐近一致性，OGF保证有界形变。仿真显示结合节点级控制器可降低1.2%-33.14%的中位累积RMDE，硬件实验显示相比固定权重一致性，OGF降低62%以上、OExpGF降低31.4%的中位编队形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统鲁棒编队控制仅在节点级调节个体机器人输入，而不修改交互拓扑，难以直接应对编队形变问题。本文旨在通过在线调整拓扑权重来直接最小化编队形变。

Method: 提出TOLD框架，包含两种策略：Online Gradient Flow (OGF) 使用无约束权重，Online Exponential Gradient Flow (OExpGF) 使用非负凸权重。采用单积分器模型在有向图上进行理论分析。

Result: 仿真中12个机器人在间歇扰动下，增强节点级控制器可降低1.2%-33.14%的中位累积RMDE。Crazyflie 2.0四旋翼硬件实验中，OGF和OExpGF分别降低62%以上和31.4%的中位编队形变。

Conclusion: TOLD框架通过在线拓扑权重自适应，能够有效降低编队形变，在理论和实验层面均验证了其有效性，优于传统固定权重一致性方法。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of robust formation control by introducing Topological Online Learning for Displacement-based (TOLD) formation control, a real-time edge-level adaptation framework. Unlike conventional node-level robust controllers that regulate individual robot inputs without modifying the interaction topology, TOLD updates the interaction topology weights online to directly minimize formation distortion. Two strategies are proposed under the TOLD formation control framework: Online Gradient Flow (OGF) with unconstrained weights and Online Exponential Gradient Flow (OExpGF) with non-negative convex weights. Theoretical analysis establishes that, for single-integrator agents over directed graphs, OExpGF guarantees asymptotic consensus, while OGF ensures bounded formation distortion. Simulations with twelve robots under intermittent disturbances show 1.2%-33.14% median cumulative Root Mean Distortion Error reduction when augmenting TOLD with node-level controllers. Hardware experiments with Crazyflie 2.0 quadrotors demonstrate over 62% (OGF) and 31.4% (OExpGF) reduction in median formation distortion compared to fixed-weight consensus.

</details>


### [128] [Sim-to-Real Betting on the E-Process: Bringing "simulators" to anytime-valid confidence sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24038)
*Yujia Chen,Bowen Weng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将模拟到现实的性能估计与赌注方法（Chen等人）和安全任意时间有效推断（Ramdas等人）相结合，利用缩放模拟器，为均值估计生成高效、可靠的证书，特别适用于机器人性能测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为机器人性能测试中模拟到现实的迁移提供一种高效、可靠的均值估计认证方法，解决传统方法在可靠性或效率上的不足。

Method: 将模拟到现实性能估计与赌注方法和安全任意时间有效推断相集成，通过缩放模拟器实现。

Result: 生成高效、可靠的均值估计证书，并在合成示例中进行了算法验证。

Conclusion: 该方法为机器人性能测试中的均值估计提供了高效的可靠证书，代码已开源。

Abstract: This note describes an integration of the sim-to-real performance estimate with betting (from Chen et al.) and the safe anytime-valid inference (from Ramdas et al.). Using the scaled simulators. The method produces efficient, reliable certificates for the mean estimate, an approach that is especially valuable in robot performance testing. This note gives a primary, self-contained account of the construction; preliminaries of the respective methods are kept at a minimum, and one shall refer to the original works for full detail. Some synthetic examples demonstrating the proposed algorithm can be found at https://github.com/ISUSAIL/Bet4Sim2Real-EProcess.

</details>


### [129] [TurboMPC: Fast, Scalable, and Differentiable Model Predictive Control on the GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24039)
*Gabriel Bravo-Palacios,Jianghan Zhang,Zachary Pestrikov,Brian Plancher,Thomas Lew*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TurboMPC是一个完全运行在GPU上的可微模型预测控制（MPC）求解器，支持状态和控制不等式约束、隐式积分器、跨时间耦合成本和松弛变量。它结合了SQP、ADMM内求解器、隐式微分和JAX-CUDA实现，在仿真中相比CPU和GPU可微求解器分别实现了最高15倍和58倍的加速，并在真实车辆上实现了基于贝叶斯优化的参数调优，同时支持超过8000个节点的规划范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人领域越来越多地依赖GPU进行并行仿真、大规模学习和神经网络推理，MPC求解器需要在此硬件上高效运行，同时保持快速、可微且兼容机器人领域常用的表达性MPC形式，以适应当前的计算范式。

Method: TurboMPC结合了序列二次规划（SQP）、交替方向乘子法（ADMM）内求解器、隐式微分以及协同设计的JAX-CUDA实现，完全在GPU上运行，支持状态和控制不等式约束、隐式积分器、跨时间耦合成本和松弛变量。

Result: 在仿真中，TurboMPC在约束规划、人形机器人模仿学习和神经网络成本函数强化学习任务中，相比最先进的CPU和GPU可微求解器分别实现了最高15倍和58倍的加速。在真实全尺寸车辆的竞速任务中，通过GPU批处理贝叶斯优化调参，获得了显著优于手动调参的驾驶性能。同时支持超过8000个节点的规划范围。

Conclusion: TurboMPC展示了GPU原生可微MPC求解器在机器人领域的巨大潜力，不仅实现了显著的加速效果，还通过贝叶斯优化实现了自动参数调优，为大规模、高性能的机器人控制提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Robotics increasingly relies on GPUs for parallel simulation, large-scale learning, and neural-network inference. For model predictive control (MPC) to scale with this paradigm, solvers must run efficiently on this hardware while remaining fast, differentiable, and compatible with expressive MPC formulations used in robotics. We present TurboMPC, a differentiable MPC solver that runs entirely on the GPU and supports state and control inequality constraints, implicit integrators, cross-time-coupled costs, and slack variables. TurboMPC combines sequential quadratic programming (SQP), an alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) inner solver, implicit differentiation, and a co-designed JAX-CUDA implementation for efficiency and ease of use. In simulation, we validate TurboMPC on constrained planning, humanoid imitation learning, and reinforcement learning with neural-network cost function tasks, achieving up to $15\times$ and $58\times$ speedups over state-of-the-art CPU and GPU differentiable solvers, respectively. We deploy TurboMPC on a full-scale car for minimum-time racing and find that batched, GPU-accelerated tuning of MPC parameters via Bayesian optimization yields significantly faster driving than a hand-tuned baseline. TurboMPC also scales to planning horizons of over $8000$ knot points while maintaining control of the vehicle. We open-source TurboMPC at: https://github.com/ToyotaResearchInstitute/turbompc

</details>


### [130] [SPACE: Enabling Learning from Cross-Robot Data Toward Generalist Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24049)
*Haeone Lee,Byeongguk Jeon,Suchae Jeong,Jian Kim,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SPACE框架，使用笛卡尔状态增量作为跨机器人的通用动作表征，通过状态预测和自适应命令执行来处理不同机器人形态、同一形态不同硬件单元以及单机器人在运行中的动力学变化，显著优于直接预测控制命令的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人动作与数据采集机器人的动力学紧密耦合，不同机器人执行相同运动需要不同动作，这种差异阻碍了跨机器人策略的训练和部署。

Method: 提出SPACE框架，包含两个组件：(i) 笛卡尔状态增量策略，预测末端执行器的几何位移；(ii) Action Adapter，将预测的笛卡尔状态增量转换为机器人特定的控制命令。跨三个层面处理动力学变化：不同形态、同一形态不同硬件单元、单机器人运行中。

Result: SPACE在跨不同形态和跨同一形态不同硬件单元的数据集上显著优于直接预测控制命令的策略，且在部署时对控制频率、物体重量和控制器增益等动力学变化保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 笛卡尔状态增量是一种有效的跨机器人通用动作表征，SPACE框架能够有效处理多层面的机器人动力学变化，实现更鲁棒的跨机器人策略学习。

Abstract: In robot learning, scaling training datasets across diverse embodiments and environments has become a dominant paradigm for learning generalizable robot policies. These policies are commonly trained via behavior cloning to imitate actions from pre-collected demonstrations. However, since robot actions are tied to the dynamics of the data collection robot, different robots may require different actions to achieve the same motion. This discrepancy hinders both policy training and deployment across diverse robots. To address this, we propose using Cartesian state delta as a universal action representation across robots, and introduce State Prediction and Adaptive Command Execution (SPACE) framework. SPACE handles robot dynamics variation at three levels: across different embodiments, across hardware units of the same embodiment, and within a single robot during operation. It consists of two components: (i) a Cartesian state delta policy that predicts geometric end-effector displacement, and (ii) Action Adapter, which converts the predicted Cartesian state delta into robot-specific control commands. Experiments show that SPACE substantially outperforms policies that directly predict control commands when learning from data collected across different embodiments and across hardware units of the same embodiment. SPACE also remains robust under dynamics shifts at deployment, including changes in control frequency, object weight, and controller gains. The project page is available at http://haeone.site/space-website/.

</details>


### [131] [MinInter: Minimizing Trajectory Interpolation During Data Augmentation for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24078)
*Qingyang Wang,Xingang Liu,Changwei Yao,Zikai Ouyang,Junwei Liu,Haibo Lu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MinInter方法，通过最小化轨迹拼接中的插值操作来生成更高质量的机器人技能学习演示数据，在MimicGen基准的12个操作任务中显著提升了数据生成成功率和策略成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹级数据增强方法通过在不同初始状态下重组专家演示来缓解高质量演示数据获取成本高的问题，但现有方法在拼接不连续轨迹片段时引入非专家的插值过渡段，降低了生成数据的质量。

Method: 提出MinInter（Minimizing Interpolation），为每个采样的初始配置选择需要最少插值操作的源演示来形成完整轨迹，从而在数据生成过程中明确最小化插值操作。

Result: 在MimicGen基准的12个操作任务（26个变体）上，MinInter一致性地提升了数据生成成功率和策略成功率，在接触密集、长时域和高方差设置中提升最为显著。相比SkillGen框架，尽管概念更简单，但实现了更高的策略成功率。

Conclusion: 最小化插值操作是提升数据增强质量的有效策略，MinInter方法简洁高效且与现有数据生成框架兼容。

Abstract: Imitation learning enables robots to acquire complex manipulation skills from demonstrations, but its effectiveness is limited by the cost of collecting high-quality data. Trajectory-level data augmentation methods alleviate this challenge by recombining expert demonstrations under varied initial states. However, such methods typically insert interpolations or other non-expert transition segments between disjoint parts, and such non-expert segments could reduce the quality of the generated data. This paper introduces Minimizing Interpolation (MinInter), an effective trajectory selection method that, for each sampled initial configuration, chooses the source demonstration requiring the least interpolation to form a complete trajectory. By explicitly minimizing interpolations during data generation, MinInter produces higher-quality synthetic demonstrations while remaining compatible with existing data generation frameworks. Experiments on 12 manipulation tasks with 26 variants from the MimicGen benchmark show that MinInter consistently improves both data generation success rates and policy success rates, with the largest gains on contact-rich, long-horizon and high-variance settings. Compared to the recent SkillGen framework, MinInter achieves higher policy success rates despite its conceptual simplicity, underscoring the value of interpolation minimization for data augmentation.

</details>


### [132] [DynaWM: Dynamics-Aware Distillation with World Model and Momentum Targets for Smooth Locomotion over Continuous Stairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24089)
*Haidong Hou,Zhangguo Yu,Hengbo Qi,Jianlin Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DynaWM框架，通过引入世界模型作为正则化器增强前向动力学感知，并采用动量目标编码器稳定知识迁移，使双足轮式机器人能够穿越连续楼梯地形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有教师-学生框架存在动力学感知表示弱化和地形几何编码不完整的问题，导致双足轮式机器人难以完成长楼梯穿越任务。

Method: 提出DynaWM框架：(1) 引入世界模型作为正则化器，增强前向动力学感知，保留完整地形几何信息并实现层次化编码可视化；(2) 采用动量目标编码器提供一致的蒸馏目标，防止非平稳教师更新导致的维度坍塌。

Result: PCA可视化和定量指标表明编码器层次化捕捉地形几何，具有更高的地形编码能力；仿真和真实硬件实验验证了该方法在连续楼梯场景中具有优越的地形适应性和运动平滑性。

Conclusion: DynaWM通过动力学感知表示学习有效解决了双足轮式机器人的长楼梯穿越难题，实现了在多样连续楼梯上的成功部署。

Abstract: Recent advances in control have enabled bipedal-wheeled robots to traverse slopes and single-step obstacles, yet long staircase traversal remains challenging as current teacher-student frameworks suffer from weakened dynamics-aware representations and incomplete terrain geometry encoding. To bridge this gap, we propose DynaWM, a dynamics-aware representation learning framework. To enhance terrain encoding capability and enable transparent assessment, we introduce a world model as a regularizer to enforce forward-dynamics awareness, preserving comprehensive terrain geometry while facilitating hierarchical encoding visualization. To stabilize knowledge transfer, we employ a momentum target encoder to provide consistent distillation targets, preventing dimensional collapse from non-stationary teacher updates. Evaluation of the learned representations through Principal Component Analysis (PCA) visualization and quantitative metrics reveals that our encoder hierarchically captures terrain geometry with higher terrain encoding capability, leading to enhanced terrain adaptability and motion smoothness. Experimental results in simulation and real hardware demonstrate that our method achieves superior terrain adaptability and motion smoothness, enabling bipedal-wheeled robots to overcome diverse continuous stairs, as shown in Fig. 1.

</details>


### [133] [NavWM: A Unified Navigation World Model for Foresight-Driven Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24101)
*Yanghong Mei,Longteng Guo,Ming-Ming Yu,Guiyu Zhao,Xingjian He,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出NavWM，一个统一导航世界模型，通过隐式世界令牌、基于锚点的多模态轨迹预测和可控视觉生成，解决了传统导航策略的短视和模式崩溃问题，在机器人导航任务上实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉导航策略存在短视决策和模式崩溃问题；现有世界模型范式将感知、生成和控制分离，未能捕捉其时空间共享动态。

Method: NavWM的核心包括：1) 隐式世界令牌，提取几何与语义先验；2) 基于锚点的多模态轨迹预测框架，生成多样化动作空间；3) 生成式世界模型作为闭环规划器，通过视觉前瞻评估并选择最优路径。

Result: 在多种机器人数据集上的实验表明，NavWM在高保真未来状态生成和零样本导航成功率方面均显著超越现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过统一感知、生成与控制的共享时空动态建模，NavWM为复杂环境下的机器人导航提供了一种鲁棒的闭环规划方案。

Abstract: Conventional visual navigation policies often struggle with myopic decision-making and mode collapse in complex environments. While world models offer a promising alternative, existing paradigms typically isolate perception, generation, and control, failing to capture their shared spatio-temporal dynamics. In this paper, we propose NavWM, a unified navigation world model that seamlessly integrates latent world reasoning, multimodal action prediction, and controllable visual generation. At its core, NavWM leverages latent world tokens to distill geometric and semantic priors, endowing the agent with robust structural understanding. To overcome the limitations of deterministic policies, we introduce an anchor-based multimodal trajectory forecasting framework that generates a diverse action space. This inherent diversity explicitly empowers the generative world model to act as a robust closed-loop planner, utilizing visual foresight to evaluate and select the optimal path. Extensive experiments across diverse robotics datasets demonstrate that NavWM significantly advances the state-of-the-art, delivering remarkable improvements in both high-fidelity future state generation and zero-shot navigation success.

</details>


### [134] [The Evaluation Cost of Task Specialization in Evolutionary Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24191)
*Paolo Leopardi,Heiko Hamann,Jonas Kuckling,Tanja Katharina Kaiser*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究多机器人系统中任务特化与通用行为在进化优化中的成本效益，发现在一定规模下，任务特化能以更低的评估预算超越通用行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明任务特化在子任务可作为构建块时更容易进化，但评估预算需要在子任务间分配，而通用行为可独占全部预算。需要量化分析特化与通用策略的成本效益权衡。

Method: 在基于物理引擎的机器人模拟器中构建觅食场景，通过进化优化对比不同规模多机器人系统下，任务特化与通用行为所需的总评估预算，研究预算与系统规模的关系。

Result: 随着多机器人系统规模增大，所需的总评估预算降低，任务特化行为能够在更低预算下超越通用行为。

Conclusion: 在多机器人系统中，系统规模越大，任务特化的成本效益优势越明显，特化策略在预算有限时更具可行性。

Abstract: Task specialization can improve the efficiency of multi-robot systems (MRSs). Previous works have investigated the emergence of task-specialist robot controllers through evolutionary optimization and have argued that task specialization is more likely to evolve when subtask behaviors are readily available as building blocks. However, the available evaluation budget must be distributed across all subtasks, whereas a single generalist behavior can exploit the entire budget for its own optimization. We present a cost-benefit analysis of evolving task-specialist versus generalist behaviors in a foraging scenario here. In a physics-based robotics simulator, we study the total evaluation budget required to evolve task-specialist behaviors that outperform generalist behaviors across MRS sizes. We show that with increasing MRS size, a lower total evaluation budget is sufficient to evolve specialists that outperform generalists.

</details>


### [135] [Grounding Generative Policies in Physics: Optimization-Guided Diffusion for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24208)
*Sabrina Bodmer,René Zurbrügg,Tifanny Portela,Hao Ma,Alexandre Didier,Marco Hutter,Colin Jones,Melanie Zeilinger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种推理时优化框架，通过将扩散引导建模为约束优化问题，在无需重新训练的前提下，在去噪过程中施加物理可行性约束，使扩散模型生成的机器人行为满足可达性、避碰和可执行性等硬件约束，在灵巧抓取和动态操作任务上分别提升任务成功率高达20和23个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型生成的高维多模态行为在任务空间上分布有效，但可能违反机器人部署的物理约束（如可达性、避碰、闭环可执行性），存在"具身鸿沟"问题，限制了策略在不同机器人本体间的零样本迁移。

Method: 将扩散模型反向过程中的采样扰动替换为优化校正，把扩散引导形式化为约束优化问题，可在采样过程中施加硬约束或软惩罚，同时保持样本接近学习到的先验分布，无需重新训练模型。

Result: 在灵巧抓取（含可达性与避碰约束）和动态操作（含控制器跟踪性约束）任务上，优化引导去噪在保持抓取质量的同时，优于投影引导和梯度引导基线，灵巧抓取任务成功率提升最高20个百分点，视觉运动操作任务提升最高23个百分点。

Conclusion: 推理时优化引导框架能有效弥合扩散模型生成行为与物理可行性之间的具身鸿沟，实现跨机器人本体的零样本任务迁移，在保持生成质量的同时显著提升约束满足率和任务成功率。

Abstract: Diffusion models sample effectively from high-dimensional, multimodal distributions, but their outputs may violate deployment constraints. For task-space robot policies, generated grasps, waypoints, or trajectories can be distributionally valid yet infeasible, violating reachability, collision-avoidance, or closed-loop executability requirements. This embodiment gap limits zero-shot deployment across robots, even when the task-space behavior itself is transferable.
  We propose an inference-time optimization framework that couples the behavior generation to physical feasibility by formulating diffusion guidance as a constrained optimization problem. Our key insight is to replace the sampling perturbation in the backward process with an optimized correction, allowing hard constraints or soft penalties to be imposed during sampling without the need to retrain the diffusion model, while keeping samples close to the learned prior.
  We evaluate the method on dexterous grasp synthesis with reachability and collision-avoidance constraints, and dynamic manipulation with controller-level trackability constraints. Across settings and robot embodiments, optimization-guided denoising matches the feasibility of projection- and gradient-guidance baselines while better preserving grasp quality, and improving controller-level executability and task success, with task success improving by up to 20pp. on dexterous grasping and 23pp. on visuomotor manipulation over the best baseline.

</details>


### [136] [RoBoSR: Structured Scene Representations for Embodied Robotic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24338)
*Kewei Hu,Wanchan Yu,Fangwen Chen,Jing Jiajian,Zimeng Li,Ying Wei,Tianhao Liu,Michael Zhang,Hanwen Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoBoSR，一种基于对象中心场景图的结构化中间表示，将 manipulation 任务建模为逐步状态转换，并构建了Manip-Cognition-1.6M数据集来联合监督场景理解、指令解释和子任务规划，在多个基准和真实世界演示中优于提示方法及经典TAMP基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身推理方法依赖演示驱动的序列偏差，在处理开放世界、长视野任务时灵活性不足，缺乏对演化状态的结构化推理能力。

Method: 提出RoBoSR中间结构表示，通过对象中心场景图将操作任务建模为基于语义的逐步状态转换，在感知-动作接口处解耦高层任务推理与原始输入，支持前提条件、效果和目标状态的结构化推理，并构建Manip-Cognition-1.6M数据集联合监督场景理解、指令解释和子任务规划。

Result: 在多个基准和真实世界演示中，该方法在零样本泛化和长视野任务上持续优于基于提示的方法和经典TAMP基线。

Conclusion: 结构化中间表示是可扩展具身推理的关键归纳偏置，能够有效提升智能体在开放世界中的因果推理能力和长视野任务规划一致性。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress, embodied reasoning under real-world variability remains challenging. Existing approaches rely on demonstration-driven sequential biases, limiting flexibility in open-ended and long-horizon tasks that require structured reasoning over evolving states.
  We introduce RoBoSR, an intermediate structural representation that formulates manipulation as step-wise state transitions over semantically grounded, object-centric scene graphs. By modeling object states and their spatial relations at the perception-action interface, RoBoSR disentangles high-level task reasoning from raw inputs and enables structured reasoning over preconditions, effects, and goal states. This representation endows the agent with causal reasoning capability, enforcing subtask dependencies and supporting coherent long-horizon task planning.
  To learn such structure-aware reasoning, we construct Manip-Cognition-1.6M, an open-world dataset that jointly supervises scene understanding, instruction interpretation, and subtask planning across diverse tasks.
  Across several benchmarks and real-world demonstrations, our method consistently outperforms prompting-based methods and classical TAMP baselines in zero-shot generalization and long-horizon tasks. The results underscore structured intermediate representations as a critical inductive bias for scalable embodied reasoning.

</details>


### [137] [SlipSense: Multimodal Sensing for Online Slip Detection in Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24350)
*Iris Szu-Yao Liu,Chien Chern Cheah,Meng Yee Michael Chuah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SlipSense框架，通过定制轻量化传感器足部结合LSTM模型，实现四足机器人早期滑移的在线力觉检测，准确率85.9%，检测分辨率较运动学方法提升3.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于运动学与本体感觉的滑移检测方法灵敏度不足，无法在灾难性失稳前检测到早期滑移，因此需要一种更敏感的力觉检测方法。

Method: 设计定制轻量化传感器足部（多模态传感），结合LSTM模型推断地面反作用力并检测滑移异常；在Unitree Go1四足机器人上部署，实现盲态在线滑移检测。

Result: 早期滑移检测平均位移24.1±6.4mm，总体准确率85.9%；检测分辨率较标准运动学基线方法提升3.3倍，准确率相对提升24%。

Conclusion: 为力觉感知的足式机器人步态自适应奠定基础，使未来控制器能估计地面摩擦并调整约束，提升系统整体稳定性。

Abstract: Legged robots rely on accurate ground interaction awareness to traverse variable terrains, such as slippery surfaces. Existing slip detection methods often rely on kinematics and proprioception, which lack the sensitivity to detect early-stage slips that occur prior to catastrophic instability. Thus, this paper presents SlipSense, a novel framework for online force-based slip detection using a custom lightweight sensorized foot for quadrupeds to detect slip. The framework integrates a multimodal sensor design with a LSTM-based model to infer ground reaction forces and detect slip-indicative anomalies during locomotion. The proposed framework is deployed on a Unitree Go1 quadruped to demonstrate blind online slip detection over a slippery terrain. Our method detects early-stage slips down to an average displacement of 24.1 +/-6.4mm with an overall accuracy of 85.9%. This represents a 3.3-fold finer detection resolution and a 24% relative accuracy improvement over a standard kinematic baseline that uses foot velocity inferred through state estimation. The work in this paper serves as a foundation for force-aware gait adaptation in legged robotic locomotion, allowing future controllers to estimate terrain friction and adjust constraints, thus improving the overall stability of the system.

</details>


### [138] [PDS Joint: A Parametric Double-Spiral Joint Tailored for Dexterous Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24377)
*Haoyang Li,Yibo Wen,Yixiang Fan,Yiheng Xu,Yufeng Yue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PDS（参数化双螺旋）柔性关节，通过阿基米德/对数螺旋模板和非对称比设计，实现手指多模态运动中的方向依赖性刚度调控；结合嵌入式电感感知与基于ArUco标记的MLP标定管线，在最大形变下将内收/外展运动状态估计误差降低41.6%；集成至开源灵巧手后成功抓取9种日常物体并完成安全人机交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有柔性关节难以同时实现大行程仿人运动、关节特定的方向依赖刚度以及可靠的本体感知，制约了柔性手在安全性和适应性方面的应用。

Method: 1) 采用参数化双螺旋（PDS）结构，分别使用阿基米德螺旋和对数螺旋模板适配不同手部关节；2) 引入非对称比参数以分别优化抓取稳定性和抗过伸刚度；3) 共设计嵌入式电感式本体感知，结合ArUco标记追踪的MLP学习标定管线实现大形变下的关节状态估计。

Result: 1) 刚度表征显示侧向支撑与非对称比呈非单调依赖关系；2) MLP映射在内收/外展最困难运动中相比传统曲线拟合误差降低41.6%；3) 集成后的灵巧手成功抓取9种日常物体并实现安全的接触丰富人机交互。

Conclusion: PDS关节通过参数化螺旋设计和非对称刚度调控，有效平衡了柔性关节的大行程运动、方向依赖性刚度与本体感知能力，为安全自适应灵巧手提供了可行的设计范式。

Abstract: Compliant joints can embed safety and adaptability into dexterous hands, but achieving large-stroke anthropomorphic motion while maintaining joint-specific, directiondependent stiffness and reliable proprioception remains challenging. This paper presents the PDS joint, a parametric doublespiral (PDS) compliant joint that enables systematic shaping of directional stiffness across multiple deformation modes, including flexion/extension, abduction/adduction, and pronation/supination. We instantiate the joint using Archimedean and logarithmic spiral templates for different hand joints and introduce an asymmetry ratio to tailor stiffness distributions for both grasp stability and hyperextension resistance. To make the joint practically usable under large deformation, we co-design embedded inductive proprioception and propose a learningbased calibration pipeline that maps raw inductive signals to joint states using ArUco-marker tracking. Experiments characterize the stiffness landscapes across geometric parameters and demonstrate a non-monotonic dependence of lateral support on asymmetry, indicating the importance of principled parameter tuning. For joint-state estimation in the most challenging abduction/adduction motion, a learned multilayer-perceptron (MLP) mapping reduces the error compared with conventional curve fitting by 41.6%. Finally, we integrate the proposed joints into an open-source dexterous hand as a demonstration platform, on which the hand grasps a set of nine everyday objects and performs safe, contact-rich human-involved interactions.

</details>


### [139] [RE4: Transformation-aware Imitation of Object Interactions Using Manipulation Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24403)
*Arsh Chawla,Rahul Shome*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RE4框架通过轻量级自监督姿态估计、操作模式感知的演示检索与变换、重规划以及演示执行四个可解释步骤，在Push-T和Robomimic基准上实现了与端到端方法相当的性能，同时保持了模型的可解释性和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模仿学习中的端到端方法（如扩散模型）性能优异但缺乏可解释性，而传统的物体中心化方法虽具备可解释性但性能不足。本文旨在构建一个兼顾性能与可解释性的操作技能学习框架。

Method: 提出RE4框架，包含四个关键步骤：(1) 轻量级自监督姿态估计，仅利用演示数据进行无模型目标物体姿态估计；(2) 操作模式感知的演示检索；(3) 模式感知的变换；(4) 重规划步骤，在保持模式约束的同时连接至检索点。最后执行变换后的演示轨迹。

Result: 在Push-T和Robomimic的基于状态和基于图像的基准上进行了评估。在图像-based Push-T的稀疏数据区域对抗性基准中展示了鲁棒性，低数据 regime 实验进一步支持了其数据效率优势。

Conclusion: 使用简单的可解释模块构建操作技能学习框架是可行的，在保持模型可解释性的同时能达到与复杂端到端方法相竞争的性能。

Abstract: Object interaction tasks have been a focus of advances in imitation learning. End-to-end methods, dominated by diffusion and flow-based variants have shown leaps in performance while sacrificing interpretability. Object-centric and pose-informed variants have had a role in learning from demonstration in manipulation tasks. In this paper, we revisit a few modern imitation learning benchmarks for object interactions, with the aim of composing a framework that repurposes principled theories of manipulation, preserving both performance and interpretability. For image observations, lightweight training is proposed for model-free pose estimation of the target object, using self-supervision over the demonstration data available for imitation learning. This information is then used to inform a manipulation mode-aware retrieval of a demonstration, a mode-aware transformation, a replan step that connects to the retrieval point while preserving mode constraints, and finally rolling out the transformed demonstration. These compose four key steps of the proposed RE4 framework, evaluated over state-based and image-based benchmarks in Push-T and Robomimic. An adversarial benchmark that evaluates sparse data regions of image-based Push-T showcases the robustness, further bolstered by indications from low-data regime experiments. The current work shows promise in using simple interpretable building blocks to learn manipulation skills.

</details>


### [140] [Legible and Intuitive Multi-modal Robot State and Intent Communication Validated in Online and Real-world Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24445)
*Tim Schreiter,Jens V. Rüppel,Andrey Rudenko,Martin Magnusson,Achim J. Lilienthal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过在线和实地实验，系统比较了移动非人形机器人在不同消息类型下，低表达性单模态LED策略与高表达性多模态（凝视、手势、语音）策略的通信可读性和直观性。结果表明，高表达性多模态通信显著优于单模态LED，且实地实验中通信可读性和用户信心均下降，揭示了虚拟到现实之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机通信的有效性对提升透明度和信任至关重要，但现有研究受限于机器人通信模态的差异、接收者理解差异以及虚拟实验与现实之间的差距，缺乏系统的大规模比较验证。

Method: 基于工业机器人标准中的规定消息类型（转向意图、注意请求、错误状态、卡住状态、正常运行状态），分别实现低表达性单模态LED策略和高表达性多模态（凝视、手势、语音）策略，并在在线和实地两种场景下进行复制实验，比较各策略的可读性和直观性。

Result: 高表达性多模态通信在可读性和直观性上显著优于单模态LED通信；与在线实验相比，实地实验的整体可读性下降，尤其是LED信号；用户对消息解读的信心在实地评估中也有所降低。

Conclusion: 高表达性多模态策略是更有效的机器人通信方式，但虚拟实验与真实场景之间存在显著差距，未来应在真实环境中验证通信策略的有效性。

Abstract: Effective robot-to-human communication can increase transparency and trust, reduce uncertainty, and contribute to safer collaboration in shared workspaces. Designing and validating an effective robot communication strategy is challenging due to the varying and often limited communication modalities across robots, differences in how diverse recipients interpret messages, and the underexplored virtual-to-real gap in studies of communication legibility. We present a systematic, large-scale comparative validation of existing communication strategies for a mobile non-humanoid robot across message types and settings (online and in-person). Based on the prescribed message types in the existing standards for industrial robots, we realize and compare a low-expressive, unimodal LED-based strategy with a highly expressive, multimodal one that leverages robotic gaze, gestures, and voice. For each strategy, we analyze the communication of a turning intention, an attention request, error status, whether the robot is stuck, and whether it is functioning normally. We evaluate these strategies in replicated online and in-person experiments. We find strong evidence that highly expressive multimodal communication is perceived as more legible and intuitive than unimodal LED-based communication. Comparing the online and real-world study findings, we observe a notable decrease in overall legibility, particularly for signaling with LEDs. Similarly, confidence in message interpretation decreases during the real-world evaluation.

</details>


### [141] [Supervise What Survives: Geometry-Guided VLA Adaptation from Synthetic Robot Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24448)
*Danze Chen,Yanzhe Chen,Qiming Huang,Zhijun Cao,Chen Gao,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出非对称保留原则：生成机器人视频仅保留几何信息（空间轨迹），丢失底层控制信号。基于此，提出GRA方法，将人类视频中的几何内容提取为2D末端执行器路点，用于监督VLA视觉骨干，而动作头仅用真实演示训练。在真实机器人任务中，GRA优于伪动作基线，缩小了与更多真实演示策略的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型需要大规模视频-动作对，但真实遥操作数据稀缺。生成机器人视频虽有扩展潜力，但现有方法从合成像素中恢复伪动作存在抽象不匹配问题：视频仅捕获几何（任务的空间轨迹/where），而真实演示捕获控制（精确电机命令/how）。视频生成过程破坏了底层控制信号，仅保留可见几何。

Method: 提出GRA方法，从源人类视频中通过姿态估计、重定向、仿真和标定投影计算未来2D末端执行器路点（几何内容），通过辅助2D头路由到VLA视觉骨干。动作头仅用真实演示训练。微调时，路点损失作为空间表征锚，防止骨干丢失几何基础。

Result: 在真实机器人任务中，GRA在匹配数据预算下优于伪动作基线，并缩小了与使用更多真实演示训练的策略之间的差距。

Conclusion: 正确路由的几何信息比恢复的动作能更可靠地桥接生成视频与机器人策略，验证了非对称保留原则的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models require large-scale video-action pairs, yet real teleoperation remains scarce. While generated robot videos offer a scalable alternative, existing methods treat them as real robot data by recovering pseudo-actions from synthesized pixels. We argue that deriving low-level control from generated visuals is a mismatched abstraction. A video captures only \emph{geometry}: the spatial trajectory representing the \emph{where} of a task. A real demonstration captures \emph{control}: the exact motor commands representing the \emph{how}. Human-to-robot video generation preserves these unequally: the visible geometry survives the generation process, while the underlying control signals are lost. This \textbf{Asymmetric Preservation Principle} dictates a clean rule: this surviving geometry should solely supervise visual perception, leaving control to real demonstrations. Following this principle, we propose \textbf{GRA} (\textbf{G}eometry-guided \textbf{R}epresentation \textbf{A}lignment), which extracts the geometric content as future 2D end-effector waypoints, computed from the source human video through pose estimation, retargeting, simulation, and calibrated projection, and routes them to the VLA vision backbone via an auxiliary 2D head. The action head is trained on real demonstrations only. During fine-tuning, the waypoint loss persists as a \textbf{spatial representation anchor} that prevents the backbone from losing its geometric grounding. On real-robot tasks, GRA outperforms pseudo-action baselines under matched data budgets and narrows the gap to policies trained with substantially more real demonstrations, suggesting that correctly routed geometry bridges generated videos to robot policies more reliably than recovered actions.

</details>


### [142] [NoContactNoWorries: Estimating Contact through Vision and Proprioception for In-Hand Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24450)
*Soham Patil,Avirup Das,Sourabh Bhosale,Spandan Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出NoContactNoWorries框架，利用Transformer融合RGB-D视觉与机器人本体感知，推断手-物交互中的二值接触状态，替代专用触觉传感器，并在仿真和真实机器人上验证了其对物体重定向任务的有效性及对新物体的泛化能力。
