<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉传感器成本高、易损坏、集成困难，而人类能够通过视觉和本体感知推断接触，受此启发，探索机器人是否也能从视觉中学习推断接触状态，提供可扩展的替代方案。

Method: 提出NoContactNoWorries框架，采用Transformer架构的多模态模型，融合RGB-D视觉与机器人本体感知（关节角度、力矩等），输出二值接触状态作为伪触觉信号，并在多个物体上训练单一接触预测模型。

Result: 该接触预测模型成功支持下游强化学习智能体完成手内物体重定向任务，并在仿真和真实机器人上验证了有效性，且能泛化到未见过的物体。

Conclusion: 从视觉和本体感知推断接触状态是可行的，可作为触觉传感器的可扩展替代方案，为灵巧操作中的接触感知提供了新思路。

Abstract: Perceiving physical contact is fundamental to dexterous manipulation. While robots often rely on dedicated hardware tactile sensors, humans exhibit a remarkable ability to infer contact by integrating visual information with an innate sense of their body's pose and movement. Inspired by this embodied perceptual skill, we investigate whether a robot can learn to infer contact from vision, an approach that also offers a scalable alternative to tactile hardware specifically for binary contact estimation, which faces practical challenges in cost, fragility, and integration. We present NoContactNoWorries, a transformer-based multimodal framework that fuses RGB-D vision with the robot's proprioception to infer binary contact states as a pseudo-tactile signal for hand-object interactions. We validate by training a single contact prediction model on multiple objects and show that the inferred contact signal supports downstream reinforcement learning agents for in-hand object reorientation, generalizing to novel objects. Experiments in both simulation and on a real-world robot validate our approach, highlighting the feasibility of inferring contact from vision and proprioception. Project Page: https://soham2560.github.io/no-contact-no-worries/

</details>


### [143] [Varying Bundle Size Reactive Multi-Task Assignment using Selective Cost Estimation for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24462)
*Niklas Dahlquist,Shridhar Velhal,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种分布式两阶段多保真度组合拍卖框架，通过低保真启发式搜索引导高保真路径规划，实现多机器人实时任务分配中的动态bundle生成，在降低计算复杂度的同时保证全局可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合拍卖方法在复杂环境中任务执行成本估算计算开销巨大，导致bundle生成的指数级复杂度使其难以用于实时反应式应用。

Method: 两阶段方法：1) 局部搜索树使用低保真启发式（如欧氏距离）快速探索bundle空间；2) 仅对最有希望的候选进行高保真路径规划（最佳优先）；3) 优化后的出价提交给中央协调器，通过集合打包问题求解确保全局可行性并最大化总效用。

Result: 在多种环境下的仿真结果表明，该框架能够提升反应式拍卖任务分配的性能，支持动态bundle大小，且不暴露智能体状态和内部成本估算模型。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了可扩展的实时多机器人任务分配，在计算效率与分配质量之间取得了有效平衡。

Abstract: This paper presents a scalable framework for multi-robot task allocation in complex environments where estimating task execution costs is computationally expensive. While combinatorial auction-based approaches offer reliable solutions, the exponential complexity of bundle generation typically renders them intractable for real-time reactive applications, particularly when accurate path planning is required for cost validation. We address this through a distributed, two-stage multi-fidelity bundle generation approach. Agents utilize a local search tree guided by a low-fidelity heuristic (such as euclidean distance) to rapidly explore the bundle space, applying high-fidelity path planning only to the most promising candidates in a best-first manner. These refined bids are then submitted to a central coordinator that solves a set packing problem to ensure global feasibility and maximize the overall utility. Simulation results in multiple environments demonstrate that the framework is able to improve the performance of reactive auction-based task allocation. Overall, the presented framework is shown to enable reactive task allocation with dynamic bundle sizes in multiple settings without exposing the agents' state and internal cost estimation models.

</details>


### [144] [FT-WBC: Learning Fault-Tolerant Whole-Body Control for Legged Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24466)
*Yudong Zhong,Pengfei Mai,Sikai Guo,Jiahang Cao,Zhihai Bi,Qiuyue Liu,Ziyan Feng,Jinni Zhou,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FT-WBC框架，通过解耦上下身策略架构、故障估计器和姿态自适应模块，实现腿臂机器人在执行器故障下的容错全身控制，在故障时维持全身稳定并尽可能保留手臂工作空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有容错控制方法主要针对纯运动（ locomotion），未能解决故障条件下腿臂机器人全身稳定性与手臂可达性相耦合的问题。

Method: 采用解耦的上下身策略架构，引入故障估计器（FE）从下身本体感知历史预测故障关节，姿态自适应模块（PAM）利用故障信息调整手臂策略生成的基础姿态规划，将不稳定姿态请求转化为安全可执行的命令。

Result: 仿真与真实实验表明，FT-WBC在弱化或锁定故障下显著提升了存活率和工作空间，且零样本迁移到真实腿臂机器人。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了执行器故障下腿臂机器人的全身控制问题，实现了容错运动操作一体化。

Abstract: Legged manipulators combine the mobility of legged platforms with the manipulation capability of robotic arms. However, arm-induced Center-of-Mass shifts and dynamic disturbances make the system more prone to instability under actuator failures, potentially leading to falls, task failures, or safety risks. Existing fault-tolerant control methods mainly focus on locomotion alone, leaving the coupled problem of whole-body stability and arm reachability in fault-tolerant loco-manipulation largely unaddressed. To bridge this gap, we propose FT-WBC, a fault-tolerant loco-manipulation framework for robust whole-body control of legged manipulators under actuator failures. FT-WBC adopts a decoupled upper- and lower-body policy architecture and introduces two key modules: a Fault Estimator (FE) and a Posture Adaptation Module (PAM). The FE predicts faulty joints from lower-body proprioceptive histories, while the PAM uses this fault information to adapt the base posture plan generated by the arm policy, converting potentially unstable posture requests into safe and executable base posture commands. Through this fault-aware posture adaptation mechanism, FT-WBC synthesizes compensatory gaits under actuator failures and preserves as much arm workspace as possible while maintaining whole-body stability. Simulation and real-world experiments show that FT-WBC significantly improves survival rate and workspace under weakening or locked failures, and transfers zero-shot to a real legged manipulator in the real world.

</details>


### [145] [G$^3$VLA: Geometric inductive bias for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24472)
*Yue Peng,Yongzhe Zhao,Artur Habuda,Khuyen Pham,Yanheng Zhu,Tran Nguyen Le,Fares Abu-Dakka,Li Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: G$^3$VLA是一种相机感知的几何模块，通过注入标定相机结构（射线嵌入、PRoPE位置编码、跨视角融合）到预训练VLA模型的视觉token流中，在不改变动作空间和模仿学习目标的前提下，显著提升多相机设置下的机器人操作性能，在LIBERO、RoboCasa24、RoboTwin2.0及真实机器人上均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的视觉token基于2D图像坐标而非机器人相机的标定几何，在多相机系统中各视角被独立处理，未能利用已知的内参和外参进行几何一致性建模，限制了空间敏感任务的表现。

Method: 提出G$^3$VLA模块：(1) 基于相机内参的条件射线嵌入；(2) 投影式位置编码（PRoPE）；(3) 双向跨视角融合。几何监督来自真实点图（可用时）或置信度门控的$\pi^3$X教师预测，无需深度传感器或人工标注。该模块可即插即用于$\pi_0$、$\pi_{0.5}$、GR00T 1.5等预训练VLA。

Result: 在LIBERO套件、RoboCasa24、RoboTwin2.0及真实机器人实验中均取得一致性能提升，在空间敏感和物体敏感任务上改进最为显著。几何感知token直接接入动作生成路径时效果最佳。

Conclusion: 向VLA注入标定相机几何结构是一种有效且通用的改进策略，跨不同VLA骨干网络和任务场景均有效，为多相机机器人操作提供了实用的几何感知增强方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have made rapid progress in generalist robot manipulation by harnessing semantic knowledge from pretrained vision-language backbones, but their visual tokens remain grounded in 2D image coordinates rather than the calibrated geometry of the robot's cameras -- a mismatch especially pronounced in multi-camera setups, where views are coupled by known intrinsics and extrinsics yet processed as independent images. We propose G$^3$VLA, a camera-aware geometric module that injects calibrated structure into the visual-token stream of a pretrained VLA without altering its action space or imitation objective, combining intrinsic-conditioned ray embeddings, projective positional encoding (PRoPE), and bidirectional cross-view fusion. Geometric supervision is provided either from ground-truth point maps when available, or from confidence-gated $π^3$X teacher predictions, requiring no depth sensors or manual annotations. Instantiated on $π_0$, G$^3$VLA yields consistent gains across the LIBERO suites, RoboCasa24, RoboTwin2.0, and real-robot settings, with the largest improvements on spatially and object-sensitive tasks. We further validate on $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T 1.5, with results suggesting that geometric transfer is most effective when geometry-aware tokens have direct access to the action generation pathway. Our project page is at https://sites.google.com/view/g3vla

</details>


### [146] [Decentralized Pose Graph Riemannian Optimization for Object-based Multi-Robot SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24489)
*Yixian Zhao,Yan Huang,Yang Xu,Liang Li,Jinming Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于黎曼优化的全分布式框架，用于面向物体的多机器人位姿图优化（PGO），通过共识机制解耦耦合估计问题，并开发了分布式近似牛顿方案以提高通信受限条件下的收敛性，在保持几何一致性的同时降低了迭代次数和通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式多机器人SLAM的位姿图优化方案通常假设通信图与物理交互拓扑紧密匹配，这在通信稀疏、间歇或时变的实际部署中具有局限性；且面向物体的多机器人SLAM中，机器人需联合估计轨迹和持久物体位姿，问题更加紧密耦合。

Method: 提出全分布式黎曼优化框架，通过共识机制解耦耦合的估计问题，支持灵活通信拓扑；进一步开发分布式近似牛顿方案，在SE(d)流形上直接操作以保持几何一致性，利用局部二阶信息加速收敛，并进行了收敛性分析和局部条件数分析。

Result: 在公开基准、大规模仿真和真实多机器人实验中，该方法在精度、运行效率、跨网络拓扑的可扩展性以及对通信故障的鲁棒性方面均优于现有方法，减少了迭代次数和通信开销而不损失估计精度。

Conclusion: 该工作为面向物体的多机器人SLAM提供了一种通信高效的分布式PGO解决方案，理论上证明了收敛到黎曼一阶驻点，并揭示了近似二阶信息相对于一阶黎曼下降的优势。

Abstract: Pose graph optimization (PGO) is a key back-end component for state estimation in networked multi-robot simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM). In object-based multi-robot SLAM, the problem becomes more tightly coupled because robots must jointly estimate both their trajectories and the poses of persistent objects observed by multiple agents. Existing decentralized solutions often assume that the communication graph closely matches the physical interaction topology, which is restrictive in realistic deployments where communication is sparse, intermittent, or time-varying. This paper presents a fully decentralized Riemannian optimization framework for object-based multi-robot PGO that decouples the coupled estimation problem via a consensus mechanism, enabling flexible communication topologies. To improve convergence under limited communication budgets, we further develop a distributed approximate-Newton scheme that exploits local second-order information while operating directly on the SE(d) manifold to preserve geometric consistency, and we establish the convergence to Riemannian first-order stationary points and provide a local condition-number analysis explaining the benefit of approximate second-order information over first-order Riemannian descent. The resulting method reduces iteration count and communication overhead without sacrificing estimation accuracy. Extensive evaluations on public benchmarks, large-scale simulations, and real-world multi-robot experiments demonstrate improved accuracy, runtime efficiency, scalability across network topologies, and robustness to communication failures.

</details>


### [147] [Explaining Failures of Cyber-Physical Systems with Actual Causality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24546)
*Khen Elimelech,Tom Yaacov,David A. Kelly,Hana Chockler,Moshe Y. Vardi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将实际因果性（actual causality）框架应用于自主网络物理系统（CPS）的失效解释，解决了理论空白，并提供了两种实用的、系统无关的解释推导算法，在神经网络控制的自动驾驶碰撞避免场景中进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代自主CPS（如自动驾驶汽车）依赖神经组件等黑盒系统，部署前无法完全验证其行为，意外失效不可避免且后果严重。为提升系统信任度并促进失效后的缓解，需要为意外行为推导解释，而现有实际因果性框架仅适用于图像分类器等简单系统。

Method: 填补实际因果性在CPS领域的理论空白，提供正确的解释推导指导；提出两种实用的、系统无关的解释推导算法，分别优先考虑解释最优性和推导效率。

Result: 在神经网络控制的自动驾驶碰撞避免场景中，对所提方法进行了演示与评估。

Conclusion: 本文首次将实际因果性框架扩展到CPS失效解释领域，通过理论贡献和两种实用算法，为复杂自主系统的行为解释提供了可行方案，兼顾了解释质量和计算效率。

Abstract: Modern autonomous Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs), such as self-driving cars, face increasingly complex demands, and yet are expected to act reliably. The black-box nature often characterizing such systems, especially those relying on neural components, makes it impossible to fully verify the system behavior prior to deployment. Unfortunately, unexpected failures-when the system does not comply with its specification-are inevitable and may have catastrophic implications. To improve trust in the system and facilitate future mitigation after a failure occurs, it is important to try to derive an explanation for the unexpected system behavior. This paper introduces the novel concept of leveraging the framework of actual causality for CPS failure explanation. Up until now, this framework was only used to derive explanations in the context of simple systems, such as image classifiers. This paper addresses the theoretical gaps and provides the guidance needed to allow for correct explanation derivation in the CPS domain. Beyond the theoretical contribution, the paper presents two novel, practical, system-agnostic explanation derivation algorithms, allowing to prioritize either explanation optimality or derivation efficiency. The approach is demonstrated and evaluated in the context of a neural-network-controlled autonomous car, designed to avoid collisions.

</details>


### [148] [Enabling Robust Cloth Manipulation via Inference-Time Simulator-in-the-Loop Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24552)
*Xin Liu,Yulin Li,Ziming Li,Pengyu Jing,Zhenhao Huang,Bingyang Zhou,Ziqiu Zeng,Siyuan Luo,Chenkun Qi,Fan Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模拟器在环优化的布料操控框架，通过FLASH可变形物体模拟器、纯合成数据训练的真实到模拟映射模块、以及结合离线蒸馏策略的在线规划方法，实现了从单张RGB图像输入到真实世界布料操控的高成功率和强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将模拟器在环优化范式从刚体操控扩展到真实世界的布料操控，解决布料操控中状态和接触复杂、难以有效建模和规划的挑战。

Method: 三个支柱：(i) 基于FLASH可变形物体模拟器的可扩展合成数据生成与推理时 rollout 管道；(ii) 纯合成数据训练的真实到模拟模块，融合预训练视觉特征与可学习规范令牌，将单张RGB观测映射为模拟兼容的布料状态；(iii) 通过稀疏网格rollout后端与先验引导的MPPI结合离线蒸馏策略轨迹进行在线规划。

Result: 在真实机器人实验中，该方法相比基线方法取得了更高的成功率和更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 模拟器在环优化范式可有效应用于真实世界的布料操控，通过合适的模拟器设计、感知模块和规划策略，能够克服布料操控中的物理复杂性挑战。

Abstract: Simulator-in-the-loop optimization offers a promising inference-time mechanism for robot manipulation. It uses a physical simulator as a backend rollout engine to evaluate candidate trajectories in parallel and refine nominal actions online, a paradigm proven effective in rigid-body manipulation where state and contact are relatively tractable. We bring this paradigm to real-world cloth manipulation from a single RGB input through three pillars. (i) We design a scalable synthetic-data generation and inference-time rollout pipeline built on FLASH, a deformable-object simulator that provides a practical balance among physical fidelity, numerical stability, and rollout efficiency. (ii) We develop a real-to-sim module, trained purely on synthetic data, that maps a single RGB observation to simulation-compatible cloth state by fusing pretrained visual features with learnable canonical tokens. (iii) We perform online planning by coupling a sparse-mesh rollout backend with prior-guided MPPI, anchored at an offline-distilled policy trajectory, preserving manipulation-relevant deformation and contact while enabling sufficient parallel rollout batches. Real-robot experiments show higher success rates and stronger robustness than baseline methods.

</details>


### [149] [ArtiTwinSplat: Interactable Digital Twin Reconstruction via Gaussian Splatting from RGB-D videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24628)
*Pranjal Mishra,René Zurbrügg,Max Wilder-Smith,Marco Hutter,Marc Pollefeys,Zuria Bauer,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ArtiTwinSplat是一个从RGB-D视频自动构建关节式、照片级逼真数字孪生的框架，无需CAD模型或人工标注，基于3D高斯泼溅和无监督关节发现，支持实时渲染和交互操作，可直接用于下游机器人规划与学习系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化真实环境中部署机器人需要准确、可交互的对象模型，但大规模构建这些模型仍然是机器人系统集成的关键瓶颈，现有方法依赖CAD模型、仿真资产或人工标注，难以规模化。

Method: 基于3D高斯泼溅保持几何保真度和光度真实感，结合无监督关节发现流水线从观测运动恢复部件结构和关节运动学，通过跟踪和优化阶段提供稳定、可查询的数字孪生。

Result: 支持实时渲染、视角控制和交互式操作，可直接在真实观测上运行，生成的数字孪生可立即被下游机器人规划和学习系统使用。

Conclusion: 该方法为数字孪生构建提供了一条实用、可扩展的路径，降低了关节式物体操作在具身AI和人机协作中的集成门槛。

Abstract: Deploying robots in unstructured real-world environments needs accurate, interactive models of the objects. Constructing these models at scale remains a critical bottleneck for robotic system integration. We present ArtiTwinSplat, a framework that automatically constructs articulated, photo-realistic digital twins of objects directly from RGB-D videos, requiring no CAD models, simulation assets, or manual annotations. Our method is built on 3D Gaussian Splatting that preserve geometric fidelity and photometric realism, coupled with an unsupervised articulation discovery pipeline that recovers part structure and joint kinematics from observed motion alone. With tracking and optimization stages our method provides stable, queryable digital twins that support real-time rendering, viewpoint control, and interactive manipulation. Unlike prior methods confined to simulation, ArtiTwinSplat operates directly on real-world observations and produces twins that are immediately usable by downstream robot planning and learning systems. This method offers a practical, scalable pathway toward digital twin construction, lowering the integration barrier for articulated object manipulation in embodied AI and human-robot collaboration contexts.

</details>


### [150] [Optimization-based Safe Trajectory Planning for Autonomous Ground Vehicle in Multi-Floor Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24631)
*Zishang Xiang,Runda Zhang,Runqi Chai,Kaiyuan Chen,Senchun Chai,Yuanqing Xia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多楼层场景的自动驾驶地面车辆轨迹规划框架，包含基于GVD和多目标算法的任务规划模块，以及采用优化方法和分层规划框架的轨迹规划模块，通过仿真验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多楼层场景下的AGV轨迹规划面临楼层间衔接、出口选择等复杂决策问题，现有方法缺乏系统化的解决方案。

Method: 框架包含两个模块：(1) 任务规划模块：基于广义Voronoi图(GVD)和多目标算法，策略性选择每层楼的出口；(2) 轨迹规划模块：采用基于优化的方法生成高质量轨迹，设计热启动分层规划框架确保快速收敛，并提出关联约束计算方法减少复杂障碍物约束数量。

Result: 通过仿真实验验证了所提出框架的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效处理多楼层场景下的AGV轨迹规划问题，任务规划与轨迹规划的协同设计提升了整体规划性能。

Abstract: The development of trajectory planning strategies for autonomous ground vehicles (AGVs) represents a prevailing research interest within the domain of intelligent transportation systems. This paper introduces a trajectory planning framework tailored for multi-floor scenarios. The framework consists of two main modules: the task planning module and the trajectory planning module. The task planning module involves a strategic selection phase, where a task planning strategy based on generalized voronoi diagrams (GVD) and multi-objective algorithms is proposed to select the floor exits for each floor. The trajectory planning module utilizes optimization-based methods to generate high-quality trajectories, and a warm-started hierarchical planning framework is designed to ensure rapid convergence. Additionally, for handling complex obstacle constraints, a correlation constraint calculation method is designed for reducing obstacle constraints in trajectory planning. Finally, the feasibility and effectiveness of the proposed framework are verified through simulations.

</details>


### [151] [Beyond Monotonic Progress: Retry-Supervised Value Learning for Robot Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24633)
*Xinyao Qin,Junjie Lu,Kaixin Wang,Chuheng Zhang,Sinjae Kang,Kimin Lee,Min Xu,Bin Liang,Jun Yang,Li Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReTVL通过利用重试事件作为稀疏监督信号，从混合质量的机器人示范中学习对错误敏感的价值函数，以改善模仿学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励和价值模型通常依赖单调进展假设，能捕捉粗略任务进度，但忽略了不完美示范中的局部执行错误和纠正行为。

Method: 结合全局进度校准与由稀疏标注的重试关键点引导的局部成对偏好学习，捕捉错误周围的局部退化-恢复结构；利用学习到的价值模型对示范片段重新加权，用于下游行为克隆。

Result: 在真实机器人操作任务上，ReTVL比基于进度的基线方法产生更细粒度的价值估计，并改善了从不完美示范中的模仿学习效果。

Conclusion: 人类示范中的错误和纠正行为并非纯噪声，而是提供了关于执行偏离可期望路径及如何恢复任务可行性的有价值证据，ReTVL能有效利用这些信息。

Abstract: Human demonstrations for robot imitation learning often contain mistakes and corrective behaviors, such as imprecise grasps, object misalignment, unstable contact, and repeated attempts. While these segments are commonly treated as noisy or suboptimal data, they provide valuable evidence about when execution deviates from a desirable path and how task feasibility can be restored. However, existing reward and value models often rely on monotonic progress assumptions, which capture coarse task advancement but may overlook local execution errors and corrective behaviors in imperfect demonstrations. In this work, we propose ReTVL (ReTry-Supervised Value Learning), a framework for learning mistake-sensitive value functions from mixed-quality robot demonstrations by leveraging retry events as sparse supervision. ReTVL captures the local degradation-and-recovery structure around mistakes by combining global progress calibration with local pairwise preference learning induced by sparsely annotated retry keypoints. The learned value model is then used to reweight demonstration chunks for downstream behavior cloning, reducing the influence of harmful execution errors while preserving useful corrective behaviors. Experiments on real-robot manipulation tasks show that ReTVL produces more fine-grained value estimates than progress-based baselines and improves imitation learning from imperfect demonstrations.

</details>


### [152] [TACTFUL: Tactile-Driven Exploration For Object Localization and Identification in Confined Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24712)
*Shivani Kamtikar,Chung Hee Kim,Camilla Tabasso,Tye Brady,Joshua Migdal,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TACTFUL，一种无需视觉的触觉探索框架，使多指机器人能自主探索受限空间、通过接触发现物体并通过触觉重建识别物体，在真实硬件上训练，无需仿真，实现77%成功率和0.015m平均重建误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统过度依赖视觉，在无视觉条件下自主触觉探索和物体识别能力严重不足，而人类可以轻松通过触觉定位和识别物体。

Method: TACTFUL框架：在真实硬件上完全训练（无仿真），使用单一策略结合全局工作空间探索与局部表面细化，通过动态奖励调度实现平衡。

Result: 达到77%的成功率，平均重建误差0.015m，在真实世界物体上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 触觉感知结合结构化学习可以作为物体级推理的有效主要模态，无需依赖视觉。

Abstract: Humans effortlessly locate and identify objects by touch alone, even without vision. In contrast, robotic systems rely heavily on vision and struggle with autonomous tactile exploration and object identification. We present TACTFUL, a vision-free tactile exploration framework that enables a multi-fingered robot to autonomously explore confined workspaces, discover objects through contact, and identify them via tactile reconstruction. Trained entirely on real hardware without simulation, our system learns a single policy that balances global workspace exploration with local surface refinement through a dynamic reward schedule. Our results demonstrate that tactile sensing, when paired with structured learning, can serve as an effective primary modality for object-level reasoning, achieving 77% success with 0.015 m average reconstruction error and outperforming baseline approaches on real-world objects.

</details>


### [153] [World Value Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24742)
*Zhihao Wang,Jianxiong Li,Yu Cui,Yuan Gao,Xianyuan Zhan,Junzhi Yu,Xiao Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出世界价值模型（WVM），将世界模型与价值估计相结合，构建通用机器人价值模型，能够在混合质量数据中准确评估数据质量，在标准基准和新提出的Suboptimal-Value-Bench上均达到SOTA性能，并有效提升策略学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人价值模型基于VLM骨干网络，缺乏时间建模能力，无法准确进行价值估计。而世界模型天然擅长时间建模和未来规划，因此探索将世界模型与价值估计结合以构建更具泛化能力的价值函数。

Method: 提出World Value Model (WVM)，将世界模型与价值估计融合，利用世界模型的时间建模和未来规划能力来实现准确的价值函数估计，并构建了包含800条次优轨迹的Suboptimal-Value-Bench基准。

Result: 在标准基准上取得SOTA的Value-Order Correlation (VOC)结果；在Suboptimal-Value-Bench上同样保持SOTA性能；在仿真和真实场景的策略学习中，WVM提升了多种策略提取方法在混合质量数据上的操作性能。

Conclusion: WVM证明了世界模型作为价值函数基础的有效性，在处理专家数据和次优数据方面均展现出鲁棒性，为从混合质量数据中学习提供了可靠的指导信号。

Abstract: Generalist value models play a pivotal role in scaling robotic policy learning from large-scale, mixed-quality data. Mathematically, accurate value estimation demands deep temporal understanding, requiring models to both ground the current belief using historical context and plan over future outcomes. However, most existing robotic value models are built on Vision-Language Model (VLM) backbones that are pretrained primarily on static or temporally sparse visual observations, lacking the requisite temporal modeling capabilities for value estimation. Unlike VLMs, world models naturally excel at temporal modeling and future planning, making them ideal foundations for learning generalizable value functions. Driven by this insight, we marry world models with value estimation to construct a new generalist robotic value model, World Value Model (WVM), that offers accurate task progressions to assess data quality. On standard benchmarks, WVM delivers state-of-the-art (SOTA) Value-Order Correlation (VOC) results. Complementing standard evaluation suites that contains only expert data, we further introduce Suboptimal-Value-Bench, a multi-embodiment benchmark consisting of 800 suboptimal trajectories with high-fidelity, human-labeled frame annotations. Our evaluations show that WVM maintains its SOTA performance on Suboptimal-Value-Bench, establishing its robustness in handling both expert and suboptimal data. When deployed for policy learning, WVM improves manipulation performance across various policy extraction approaches in both simulated and real-world deployment, providing robust guidance for learning from mixed-quality data.

</details>


### [154] [Vision-Language Model Reasoning for Contextual Semantic Mapping in Intralogistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24814)
*Marvin Rüdt,Hao Pang,Constantin Enke,Zäzilia Seibold,Kai Furmans*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合SLAM几何建图、SAM实例分割、实例聚类和VLM多视角推理的上下文语义建图管线，能在零样本、开放词汇条件下推断物体的可移动性等上下文属性，在语义分类和可移动性估计上分别达到98.93% mIoU和89.17% mAcc。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主移动机器人在内部物流环境中依赖几何地图进行定位和导航，但缺乏对物体及其上下文属性的语义理解，无法支持更智能的环境交互。

Method: 提出一种上下文语义建图管线，包含四个模块：基于SLAM的几何建图、基于SAM的实例分割、实例聚类、以及VLM多视角推理。通过聚合多视角观测并以零样本、开放词汇方式查询VLM，推断物体的上下文属性（如可移动性），无需任务特定训练或预定义类别。

Result: 语义分类达到98.93% mIoU，物体可移动性估计达到89.17% mAcc。组件分析表明VLM推理是上下文理解的主要瓶颈，实例聚类是全景性能的主要限制。

Conclusion: 生成的语义地图支持上下文感知过滤和动态内部物流环境中的鲁棒导航，验证了多模态大模型在机器人语义建图中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots operating in intralogistics environments rely on geometric maps for localization and navigation, but lack semantic understanding of objects and their contextual properties. We present a contextual semantic mapping pipeline that combines SLAM-based geometric mapping, SAM-based instance segmentation, instance clustering, and VLM multi-view reasoning to produce a contextual semantic map representation encoding geometric structure, object class, and object movability. By aggregating observations across multiple viewpoints and querying a VLM in a zero-shot, open-vocabulary setting, the pipeline infers contextual object properties--here demonstrated through movability--without requiring task-specific training or predefined object categories. We evaluate three VLMs under two prompting strategies and conduct a component-wise analysis of the pipeline. The proposed pipeline achieves 98.93 % mIoU for semantic classification and 89.17 % mAcc for object movability estimation. Component analysis identifies VLM reasoning as the primary bottleneck for contextual understanding and instance clustering as the main limitation for panoptic performance. The resulting semantic map supports context-aware filtering and robust navigation in dynamic intralogistics environments.

</details>


### [155] [InSight: Self-Guided Skill Acquisition via Steerable VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24884)
*Maggie Wang,Lars Osterberg,Stephen Tian,Ola Shorinwa,Jiajun Wu,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: InSight提出一种框架，通过将VLA模型在原始动作层面变得可控（如“将夹爪移动到碗边”），实现自主技能获取。该框架包含自动化分割管道（利用VLM将演示分解为带标签的原始动作）和VLM引导的数据飞轮（自动识别缺失原始动作、自主尝试演示并集成成功数据）。无需人类演示即可在模拟和真实任务中学习新技能，并可组合执行长时域任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的能力受限于训练数据中的技能范围，无法自主获取训练数据中未包含的新技能。InSight旨在通过让VLA模型在原始动作层面变得可控制，从而实现持续自主的技能获取。

Method: (1) 自动化分割管道：利用VLM对演示进行计划分解，结合末端执行器位姿，将演示分割为带标签的原始动作，使VLA获得原始动作可控性。(2) VLM引导的数据飞轮：识别完成任务所需的缺失原始动作，由VLM提出底层控制策略自主尝试演示，自动标注、存储并整合成功演示到VLA训练集。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界的多种操作任务（如翻转方块、关闭抽屉、扫除、扭转、倾倒）中均取得有效成果，且无需任何目标技能的人类演示。学习到的原始动作可组合执行新的长时域任务。

Conclusion: 原始动作可控性为VLA策略的持续技能获取提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can learn manipulation skills from demonstrations, but their capabilities are bounded by the skills in the training data. We present InSight, a framework that unlocks autonomous skill acquisition by rendering VLAs steerable at the primitive-action level (e.g., "move gripper to the bowl", "lift upward", "pour the bottle"). InSight consists of two primary stages: (1) an automated segmentation pipeline that partitions demonstrations into labeled primitives via VLM plan decomposition and end-effector poses to enable VLA primitive steerability, and (2) a VLM-guided data flywheel that identifies missing primitives required to accomplish a novel task, autonomously attempts demonstrations of the missing primitives with VLM-proposed low-level control, and automatically labels, stores, and integrates successful demonstrations into the VLA training set. We evaluate InSight across simulation and real-world manipulation tasks, including block flipping, drawer closing, sweeping, twisting, and pouring, without any human demonstrations of these target skills. Once learned, these primitives can be composed to execute novel, long-horizon tasks without additional human demonstrations. Our findings demonstrate that primitive steerability provides a practical foundation for continual skill acquisition in VLA policies. Project website: https://insight-vla.github.io.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [RIFT-Bench: Dynamic Red-teaming For Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23927)
*Yarin Yerushalmi Levi,Roy Betser,Amit Giloni,Lidor Erez,Itay Gershon,Oren Rachmil,Sindhu Padakandla,Roman Vainshtein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RIFT-Bench，一种基于图表示驱动的动态红队测试方法，用于统一评估异构智能体AI系统的安全性，涵盖45个不同系统的实验验证了其泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估方法通常绑定特定实现或领域，缺乏跨异构智能体系统的统一比较框架，难以全面覆盖大语言模型驱动的自主决策系统所暴露的新型攻击面。

Method: RIFT-Bench采用层次化图表示，通过两个自动化阶段运作：Discovery阶段提取系统结构，Scanning阶段部署自适应对抗攻击并生成综合评估报告，支持多种攻击向量和目标的可动态调整的对抗探针。

Result: 在45个涵盖广泛实现类型的智能体系统上进行实验，证明了该方法能有效泛化到异构智能体架构，并支持对缓解策略的直接评估。

Conclusion: RIFT-Bench为智能体AI系统的安全评估提供了可扩展的基础框架，统一了跨不同架构的安全评测。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems powered by large language models (LLMs) are rapidly evolving into autonomous decision-making systems, exposing attack vectors beyond those of traditional LLM vulnerabilities. Existing security evaluations are often tied to specific implementations or domains, limiting unified comparison across heterogeneous systems. To address this gap, we introduce RIFT-Bench, a graph representation-driven methodology for dynamic red-teaming that enables unified evaluations across diverse agentic architectures. Building on a novel hierarchical representation, RIFT-Bench operates in two automated phases: Discovery, which extracts system structure, and Scanning, which deploys adaptive adversarial attacks and produces a comprehensive evaluation report. It evaluates the examined system itself, leveraging a broad set of dynamically adaptable adversarial probes across diverse attack vectors and objectives. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed evaluation pipeline across 45 agentic systems spanning a diverse range of implementations, showing that the approach generalizes effectively to heterogeneous agentic architectures. Beyond systems and attacks, RIFT-Bench also supports direct evaluation of mitigation strategies. These key capabilities make RIFT-Bench a scalable foundation for security evaluation of agentic AI systems.

</details>


### [157] [Neuro-Symbolic Drive: Rule-Grounded Faithful Reasoning for Driving VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23938)
*Xiangbo Gao,Xiukun Huang,Boyu Lu,Junge Zhang,Mengjie Mao,Jiachen Li,Wei Xiong,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Neuro-Symbolic Drive框架，通过从经典规则规划器中提取规则约束的推理轨迹来监督驾驶VLA模型，使推理与运动生成在结构上耦合，在仿真基准上显著降低了ADE和miss rate。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶VLA模型虽引入CoT推理，但中间推理步骤缺乏与规划动作因果相连的决策语义，导致推理与运动生成脱节。

Method: 利用规则规划器作为可执行推理引擎，在仿真中捕获其内部决策轨迹（包括安全约束推理、候选动作搜索、最终轨迹选择），将其序列化为结构化规则推理文本，与轨迹配对微调Qwen3.5-4B作为驾驶VLA。

Result: 三摄像头感知下ADE@3s从0.47降至0.26，miss rate从8.30%降至6.40%；八摄像头感知下ADE@3s从0.54降至0.26，miss rate从10.13%降至5.99%。

Conclusion: 将神经符号规划逻辑转化为结构化监督信号，能够有效提升驾驶VLA模型的推理与运动生成一致性。

Abstract: Driving VLA models incorporating Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning are attractive because they leverage pretrained VLM representations and expose intermediate decisions in natural language, yet current rationales often lack the step-by-step decision semantics needed to keep the rationale causally connected to the planned motion. We introduce Neuro-Symbolic Drive, a neuro-symbolic driving framework that supervises a driving VLA with rule-grounded reasoning traces extracted directly from classical rule-based planners. Our key observation is that rule-based planners are symbolic AI systems that already function as executable reasoning engines: they reason about active safety constraints, search over candidate maneuvers, and select a final trajectory. We instrument these planners in simulation to capture both the executed trajectory and the internal decision trace at each rule-evaluation step. Each trace is serialized into structured rule-grounded reasoning and paired with the trajectory to fine-tune Qwen3.5-4B as a driving VLA. Because these traces are derived directly from the planner states that determine the action, they ensure reasoning is structurally coupled to motion generation by construction, rather than by post-hoc alignment. On our simulator-generated benchmark, detailed rule-grounded reasoning reduces ADE@3s from 0.47 to 0.26 and miss rate from 8.30% to 6.40% under three-camera perception, and from 0.54 to 0.26 and 10.13% to 5.99% under eight-camera perception. Neuro-Symbolic Drive thus converts neuro-symbolic planning logic into structured supervision. Code base: https://github.com/XiangboGaoBarry/Neural-Symbolic-Drive.

</details>


### [158] [Critique of Agent Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23991)
*Eric Xing,Mingkai Deng,Jinyu Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文区分了“代理性”（agentic）系统（依赖外部脚手架）与“主体性”（agentive）系统（内化目标、身份、决策、自我调节和学习），提出GIC架构作为通用主体模型，并讨论了高自主性主体系统的可审计性、可控性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM系统以“编码代理”“AI共同科学家”等主体化工具形式兴起，同时伴随AI失控等存在性担忧，厘清自动化与主体性之间的界限变得至关重要，既为构建有能力的系统，也为理解是否及如何产生担忧。

Method: 基于笛卡尔关于独立思想的主体性根基与科幻作品中的自主存在描述，沿目标（goal）、身份（identity）、决策（decision-making）、自我调节（self-regulation）、学习（learning）五个维度调查并分析当前AI代理架构。

Result: 提出真正的主体性要求上述结构内化于系统自身而非依赖外部脚手架；区分“代理性”（agentic）系统（能力源于工程化工作流）与“主体性”（agentive）系统（包括社会交互在内的能力内生涌现）；提出GIC（Goal-Identity-Configurator）架构，结合层次化目标分解、身份演化、基于独立世界模型的模拟推理、习得的自我调节以及从真实和模拟经验中的自我导向学习。

Conclusion: 对具备更高自主性和“主体性”但仍处于人类监督下的系统，分享了关于可审计性、可控性与安全性的见解。

Abstract: What is an agent? What constitutes agency? With the rise of Large Language Model (LLM) systems marketed as ``coding agents'', ``AI co-scientists'', and other ``agentic" tools that promise to drive up productivity, and at the same time, ``existential" concerns such as AI escaping human control with destructive power under a speculative ``machine agency" against humans, it has become essential to clarify where automation ends and agency begins, both for building capable systems and for understanding whether and what to fear. Drawing on Descartes' grounding of agency in independent thought, and on portrayals of autonomous beings in science fiction, we survey the current landscape of AI agents, and analyze agent architectures along five dimensions: goal, identity, decision-making, self-regulation, and learning. Specifically, we argue that genuine agency requires these structures to be \emph{internalized within the system itself} rather than assembled through external scaffolding. This distinction between \emph{agentic} systems, whose competence resides in engineered workflows, and \emph{agentive} systems, whose capabilities (including social interaction) arise endogenously, defines the boundary between systems designed for prescribed tasks, and those capable of operating in the open world with true autonomy. Building on this analysis, we propose the Goal-Identity-Configurator (GIC) architecture for a general-purpose agent model, combining hierarchical goal decomposition, identity evolution, simulative reasoning grounded in a separately trained world model, learned self-regulation, and self-directed learning from both real and simulated experience. Furthermore, we share insight on the auditability, controllability, and safety of agentive systems that possess greater autonomy and ``agency", but remain under human oversight.

</details>


### [159] [Safe and Generalizable Hierarchical Multi-Agent RL via Constraint Manifold Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24010)
*Zihao Guo,Jianing Zhao,Ling Li,Hao Liang,Giuseppe Loianno,Yali Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种分层多智能体强化学习框架，通过低层约束流形强制执行硬安全约束，同时高层策略学习实现有效协调，在保证理论安全性的同时实现了接近完美的安全率和竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在根本性权衡：基于学习的方法经验性能强但缺乏安全保证，而控制理论方法能保证安全但过于保守低效。亟需一种既能提供理论安全保证又能实现高效协调的多智能体框架。

Method: 提出分层多智能体强化学习框架：低层通过约束流形在温和假设下强制执行硬安全约束，高层通过策略学习实现智能体间的有效协调。该框架在理论上提供多智能体环境下的安全保证，并产生平稳的学习动态。

Result: 经验上，该方法在保持接近完美安全率的同时取得了竞争性性能，并能有效泛化到不同数量的智能体和障碍物场景。

Conclusion: 所提框架成功弥合了学习方法和控制理论方法之间的鸿沟，同时实现了理论安全保证、稳定高效的训练以及良好的泛化能力，适用于安全关键的多智能体协同应用。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are widely used in safety-critical applications that require coordinated behavior under strict safety constraints. Existing approaches face a fundamental trade-off: learning-based methods achieve strong empirical performance but lack theoretical safety guarantees, while control-theoretic methods enforce safety but often lead to overly conservative and inefficient behaviors. We propose a hierarchical multi-agent reinforcement learning framework that enforces hard safety constraints under mild assumptions at low level via a constraint manifold, while enabling effective coordination through high-level policy learning. Our approach provides theoretical safety guarantees in the multi-agent setting and yields stationary learning dynamics, thereby enabling stable and efficient training. Empirically, our method achieves competitive performance while maintaining nearly perfect safety rates, and generalizes effectively to varying numbers of agents and obstacles.

</details>


### [160] [Reinforcement Learning Towards Broadly and Persistently Beneficial Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24014)
*Akshay V. Jagadeesh,Rahul K. Arora,Khaled Saab,Ali Malik,Mikhail Trofimov,Foivos Tsimpourlas,Johannes Heidecke,Karan Singhal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究验证了在真实场景中通过强化学习训练有益行为（如诚实、公平、风险意识等），能够产生广泛且持久的对齐泛化效果。该RL干预在80%以上的分布外基准上提升了对齐表现，单一领域的训练（如健康）也能泛化到其他领域，并增强模型对对抗性提示和有害微调的抵抗能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在多样化和高风险场景中部署时，模型对齐必须泛化到训练任务和领域之外。特别是在强化学习中，可能出现奖励破解、欺骗等意外错位问题，因此需要研究如何通过RL训练有益行为来实现广泛且持久的对齐泛化。

Method: 构建了一个覆盖健康、科学、教育等多个领域的真实场景数据集，用于衡量和训练有益特质（如诚实、公平、风险意识、可修正性）。在此数据集上对模型进行RL训练，并在50多个独立的对齐与有益行为基准上评估效果。同时研究了单一领域（健康）训练后的跨领域对齐泛化，以及模型在对抗性提示和有害微调下的对齐持久性。

Result: 与计算量匹配的基线相比，有益特质RL在超过80%的分布外基准上提升了性能。单一领域（健康）的RL干预也能在非健康领域的对齐评估中产生广泛改进，包括减少奖励破解、欺骗和一般性错位。模型还表现出更强的对齐持久性，对对抗性提示和有害微调具有更高的抵抗能力。

Conclusion: 在真实领域中通过RL强化有益行为，可以训练出对人类福祉更具鲁棒对齐性的模型。但需进一步研究以分离这些效应的来源。

Abstract: As AI systems are deployed across increasingly diverse and high-stakes settings, model alignment must generalize beyond the tasks and domains seen during training. This is especially important for reinforcement learning (RL), which can introduce unexpected misalignment through reward hacking, deception, or other unintended strategies. We study whether RL on beneficial behavior, instantiated in realistic domains, can produce broad and persistent alignment generalization beyond the training distribution. We construct a dataset of realistic situations designed to measure and train beneficial traits, such as truthfulness, fairness, risk awareness, and corrigibility, spanning varied domains, including health, science, and education. We then train models with RL on this dataset and evaluate them on more than 50 independent benchmarks of alignment and beneficial behavior. Compared to a compute-matched baseline, beneficial trait RL improves performance on over 80% of these out-of-distribution benchmarks. We observe substantial out-of-distribution alignment transfer: a beneficial-behavior RL intervention entirely limited to one domain, health, produces broad improvements on non-health alignment evaluations, including reduced reward hacking, deception, and general misalignment. Finally, we study alignment persistence: whether behavior remains robustly aligned under attempts to steer models towards misalignment. Models trained with beneficial trait RL show improved persistence, including greater resistance to adversarial prompting and harmful finetuning; further work is required to isolate the sources of these effects. These results suggest that RL to reinforce beneficial behavior in realistic domains can produce models that are more robustly aligned with human flourishing.

</details>


### [161] [Can Language Model Agents be Helpful Circuit Explainers in Mechanistic Interpretability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24026)
*Ayan Antik Khan,Harsh Kohli,Yuekun Yao,Huan Sun,Ziyu Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了HyVE框架和AgenticInterpBench基准，用于自动化解释已定位的神经网络电路组件，发现LM智能体在电路解释上潜力巨大，但可靠验证仍是主要障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性在自动定位电路方面取得了进展，但解释这些已定位组件的功能仍然费时费力且难以标准化，亟需自动化解释方法。

Method: 提出了AgenticInterpBench基准（84个半合成变压器电路+163个组件级标注）和HyVE框架（假设-验证-解释），通过观察-假设生成-因果验证的迭代循环进行组件级和电路级解释。

Result: 在四种LM骨干模型上，HyVE能恢复有用的组件级和任务级解释，但没有一个骨干模型在所有方面表现最佳。强模型通常能形成基于观测的假设，失败更多出现在验证循环后期（不完整验证计划、代码执行错误或未解决的假设）。在Llama-3-8B的算术电路案例中验证了方法可扩展到自然训练模型。

Conclusion: LM智能体作为电路解释器具有应用前景，但可靠的验证仍是关键挑战。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has made substantial progress in automatically localizing circuits, but explaining what localized components do remains labor-intensive and difficult to standardize. In this work, we study whether language model (LM) agents can assist with this explanation problem once a circuit has already been identified. We introduce AgenticInterpBench, a benchmark for circuit explanation built from 84 semi-synthetic transformer circuits with 163 component-level annotations. We propose HyVE (Hypothesize, Validate, Explain), an agentic explainer that analyzes each component through an iterative loop of observation, hypothesis generation, and causal validation, eventually producing a component-level explanation and a circuit-level task description. Across four LM backbones, HyVE recovers useful component- and task-level explanations, but no backbone is uniformly best. Our analysis shows that strong backbones usually form observation-grounded hypotheses, while failures more often arise later in the validation loop, through incomplete validation plans, code execution errors, or unresolved hypotheses. A case study on an arithmetic circuit in Llama-3-8B shows that the same formulation can extend beyond semi-synthetic benchmarks to naturally trained models. Overall, LM agents are promising circuit explainers, but reliable validation remains the key obstacle.

</details>


### [162] [Breaking the Filter Bubble: A Semantic Pareto-DQN Framework for Multi-Objective Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24042)
*Cláudio Lúcio Do Val Lopes,Lucca Machado da Silva,André de Oliveira Brandão*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多目标强化学习框架（Pareto-DQN），将推荐系统建模为语义多目标马尔可夫决策过程，通过超体积动作选择在用户参与度、信息多样性和提供者公平性之间进行帕累托优化，实验表明该方法在不显著牺牲参与度的前提下有效提升了社会性目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统单调优化即时用户参与度，导致过滤气泡和语义同质化；标准单目标模型（如DQN）无法处理平台留存与社会价值（信息多样性、提供者公平性）之间的权衡。

Method: 提出多目标强化学习框架，将推荐形式化为语义多目标MDP，结合高保真语义嵌入与Pareto-DQN智能体，将参与度、多样性和公平性作为独立不可聚合的奖励信号，使用基于超体积（hypervolume）的动作选择来避免静态奖励标量化的缺陷。

Result: 在MovieLens小数据集上的实验表明，Pareto-DQN通过维持高状态-轨迹方差，有效绘制了帕累托前沿，在辅助社会目标上取得显著提升，同时仅对参与度产生边际影响，打破了语义崩溃的反馈循环。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建内在对齐的负责任的推荐系统提供了可行路径，实现了平台参与度与社会价值之间的平衡优化。

Abstract: Recommender systems often induce filter bubbles and semantic homogenization by monolithically optimizing for immediate user engagement. Standard single-objective models, including traditional Deep Q-Networks, are ill-equipped to navigate the trade-offs between platform retention and critical societal values like information diversity and provider fairness. To address these limitations, we introduce a multi-objective reinforcement learning framework that formalizes recommendation as a semantic multi-objective Markov decision process. By integrating high-fidelity semantic embeddings with a Pareto-DQN agent, our architecture treats engagement, diversity, and fairness as distinct, non-aggregable reward signals, avoiding the pitfalls of static reward scalarization. Empirical evaluations on the MovieLens small dataset shows that our hypervolume based action selection disrupts the feedback loops responsible for semantic collapse. By sustaining high state-trajectory variance, the Pareto-DQN effectively maps the Pareto frontier, achieving gains in auxiliary societal objectives with only marginal impacts on engagement. This work provides a path toward intrinsically aligned, responsible recommender systems.

</details>


### [163] [Ensemble Feature Selection and Harris Hawks Optimization for Explainable Mental Health Risk Prediction in Female Sex Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24047)
*Ahnaf Atef Choudhury,Md. Parvej Hoque Palash,Shahriar Siddique Ayon,Ramkrishna Saha,Abdullah Al Mamun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合ANOVA与互信息的集成特征选择策略，并结合Harris Hawks优化逻辑回归的混合预测模型，用于预测女性性工作者的抑郁风险。模型在3005名FSWs数据上达到95.78%准确率、95.77% F1分数和0.96 AUC，识别出创伤后应激、客户暴力和职业因素为主要风险因素，并通过可解释AI提供决策依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 女性性工作者面临抑郁等严重心理健康问题，现有机器学习模型难以捕捉该边缘群体特有的高维复杂风险模式，亟需更有效的预测工具。

Method: 采用基于ANOVA与互信息的集成特征选择策略筛选关键特征，使用Harris Hawks优化算法调优逻辑回归模型，并结合可解释AI方法解释预测结果。

Result: 在3005名FSWs数据上，模型准确率95.78%、F1分数95.77%、AUC为0.96，优于传统分类器；识别出创伤后应激、客户暴力和职业因素为抑郁主要风险因素。

Conclusion: 该混合模型弥合了传统方法与机器学习之间的差距，为弱势群体提供了可解释的早期心理援助工具，支持循证的针对性心理社会干预与健康规划。

Abstract: One of the significant mental health issues affecting female sex workers (FSWs) is mental disorders, especially depression. Exposure to violence, stigma, and economic hardship further increases their psychological risk. Current machine learning (ML) models are typically ineffective at capturing the high-dimensional and complex risk patterns that exist in this marginalized group. This paper suggests a hybrid predictive model that merges an ensemble feature selection strategy using ANOVA and mutual information and Harris Hawks optimization-tuned logistic regression and represents a new application of swarm intelligence to predict mental health in vulnerable groups. The explainable AI (XAI) methods can be used to understand the factors of trauma associated with model predictions. When applied to a group of 3,005 FSWs, it can be seen that the proposed model is more effective than traditional classifiers, with an accuracy of 95.78%, an F1 score of 95.77%, and an AUC of 0.96, and identifying post-traumatic stress, client-related violence, and occupational factors as major contributors to depression. This work bridges the gaps between conventional and ML approaches to develop an XAI tool that enables vulnerable groups to receive early assistance, evidence-based targeted psychosocial care, and health planning.

</details>


### [164] [Beyond Trajectory Imitation: Strategy-Guided Policy Optimization for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24064)
*Tianyuan Shi,Canbin Huang,Bei Li,Xin Chen,Xiaojun Quan,Jingang Wang,Qifan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出策略引导策略优化（SGPO），通过可复用的策略蒸馏替代实例级轨迹模仿，将强模型推理能力转移到弱模型。SGPO提取结构化策略描述，结合前向KL散度与自适应实例级加权，在四个数学基准上优于SFT、on-policy RL和混合策略基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识蒸馏方法从强到弱模型的推理能力迁移通常采用模仿特定解轨迹的方式，这实质上转移的是"回答什么"而非"如何推理"。这种轨迹级模仿导致模型记忆实例特定步骤而非获取可迁移的问题解决技能，限制了模型对新颖问题的泛化能力。

Method: SGPO包含三个核心组件：(1) 从强模型响应中提取结构化策略描述；(2) 对每个问题构建自主轨迹（无策略引导）和策略引导轨迹，实现有无策略引导的行为直接对比；(3) 采用token级前向KL散度选择性转移策略条件化引起的分布偏移，并通过近端约束保证稳定性；同时设计自适应实例级加权机制，在自主探索不足时加强引导，在模型能力增长时减弱引导。

Result: 在四个数学基准上跨两个模型家族的实验表明，SGPO一致优于SFT、on-policy RL和混合策略基线。在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上将平均分数提升2.2分（相较于最强基线）。分析表明前向KL目标提供了内在选择性的蒸馏信号，优于直接轨迹模仿；策略蒸馏与基础模型能力呈互补缩放关系。

Conclusion: SGPO通过策略级蒸馏替代轨迹级模仿，有效解决了从强到弱模型推理能力迁移中的泛化问题。前向KL散度的选择性蒸馏信号和自适应加权机制共同实现了更高效的推理能力迁移，且该方法与基础模型能力存在互补缩放效应。

Abstract: Distilling reasoning capabilities from strong to weak language models typically involves imitating specific solution trajectories, effectively transferring what to answer rather than how to reason. This trajectory-level imitation encourages memorization of instance-specific steps rather than acquisition of transferable problem-solving skills, limiting generalization to novel problems. We propose Strategy-Guided Policy Optimization (SGPO), which replaces instance-level trajectory imitation with reusable strategy distillation. SGPO extracts structured strategy descriptions from strong-model responses and, for each problem, constructs both autonomous and strategy-guided trajectories to enable direct comparison of the model's behavior with and without strategic guidance. The framework then addresses two key questions. For how to distill, a token-level forward-KL objective selectively transfers the distributional shift induced by strategy conditioning into the unguided policy, with proximal constraints ensuring stability. For when to distill, adaptive instance-level weighting strengthens guidance when autonomous exploration falls short and reduces it as the model's own competence grows. Experiments on four mathematical benchmarks across two model families show that SGPO consistently outperforms SFT, on-policy RL, and hybrid-policy baselines, improving the average score by 2.2 points over the strongest baseline on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct. Analysis reveals that the forward-KL objective provides an inherently selective distillation signal that outperforms direct trajectory imitation, and that strategy distillation exhibits complementary scaling with base model capability.

</details>


### [165] [Exploring Academic Influence of Algorithms by Co-occurrence Network Based on Full-text of Academic Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24099)
*Yuzhuo Wang,Chengzhi Zhang,Min Song,Seong Deok Kim,Youngsoo Ko,Juhee Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究基于40余年间NLP领域论文全文，构建了大规模算法共现网络，从网络视角分析算法的群体影响力，发现算法网络具有复杂网络特征，经典高性能算法及跨时期算法具有较高影响力，算法影响力下降时首先失去核心网络位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常孤立评估单个算法的影响力，忽视了算法之间通过相互连接形成的群体影响力，缺乏从网络视角对算法影响力的分析。

Method: 利用深度学习模型从论文全文中提取算法实体，构建总体共现网络、累积共现网络和年度共现网络，采用多种中心性指标评估算法的群体影响力。

Result: 算法网络呈现复杂网络典型特征，约二十年间连接日益密集；经典高性能算法和处于不同研究时期交叉点的算法具有高流行度、控制力、中心性和均衡影响力；算法影响力下降时，首先失去核心网络位置，随后与其他算法的关联减弱。

Conclusion: 本研究首次大规模分析算法共现网络，覆盖40余年学术文献，提供了算法影响力的时间和结构视角，为未来算法、学者、任务网络研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Algorithms have become central to scientific research in the era of artificial intelligence (AI). Although algorithm mentions in papers are often used to indicate popularity and influence, existing studies usually evaluate individual algorithms in isolation and pay limited attention to the collective influence formed through their interconnections. This study constructs large-scale algorithm co-occurrence networks in natural language processing (NLP) based on the full text of academic papers and investigates algorithm influence from a network perspective. Using deep learning models, we extract algorithm entities and build overall, cumulative, and annual co-occurrence networks. We analyze their structural characteristics and apply multiple centrality measures to assess the group influence of algorithms across the whole field and over time. The results show that algorithm networks display typical features of complex networks, with increasingly dense connections developing over approximately two decades. Classic, high-performing algorithms and those located at the intersections of different research periods tend to have high popularity, control, centrality, and balanced influence. When the influence of an algorithm declines, it usually loses its core network position first, followed by weaker associations with other algorithms. This study is the first large-scale analysis of algorithm co-occurrence networks. Covering more than four decades of academic publications, it provides a temporal and structural view of algorithm influence and offers a foundation for future research on networks linking algorithms, scholars, and tasks.

</details>


### [166] [ReMMD: Realistic Multilingual Multi-Image Agentic Verification for Multimodal Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24112)
*Chenhao Dang,Dantong Zhu,Jun Yang,Conghui He,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReMMD 提出了一个面向现实多语言多图像场景的多模态虚假信息检测框架，包含包含500个样本、2756张图像、五种语言、五类真值标签的基准数据集ReMMDBench，以及一个基于持久记忆的智能体验证器ReMMD-Agent，在GPT-5.2上达到41.80%准确率和39.12%宏F1，并分别降低17.5%和79.9%的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态虚假信息检测基准和方法与真实场景严重不匹配：通常局限于短标题、单张图像、二元标签或单一操纵来源，且基于智能体的验证在现实证据搜索下成本高昂。

Method: 提出ReMMD框架，包括：（1）ReMMDBench基准，涵盖多语言、多图像、五类真值标签、八类失真标签、证据来源和推理依据；（2）ReMMD-Agent验证器，采用持久记忆机制，将帖子分解为原子化要点、构建可复用证据集、预测结构化的L1/L2/L3输出。

Result: 在多种闭源系统、开源LVLM、MMD-Agent和T2-Agent对比中，ReMMD-Agent取得最优的五分类真值检测性能，GPT-5.2上准确率41.80%、宏F1 39.12%，成本相比MMD-Agent降低17.5%、相比T2-Agent降低79.9%。

Conclusion: ReMMD框架有效填补了现有方法在现实多语言多图像场景下的空白，通过持久记忆和原子化解构显著提升了检测性能与成本效率。

Abstract: Multimodal misinformation detection is increasingly important because viral posts now combine long multilingual narratives, several images, mixed provenance, and subtle text--image framing errors. Existing benchmarks and methods remain poorly matched to this setting: they usually isolate short captions, single images, binary labels, or one manipulation source, while agentic verification remains costly under realistic evidence search. We present ReMMD, a realistic multilingual multi-image agentic verification framework for multimodal misinformation detection. ReMMD includes ReMMDBench, a real-world multimodal misinformation detection benchmark with 500 samples, 2,756 images, five monolingual languages, two cross-lingual settings, three text-length tiers, multi-image posts, five-way veracity labels, eight distortion labels, evidence provenance, and rationales. It also includes ReMMD-Agent, a persistent-memory verifier that decomposes posts into atomic points, builds a reusable evidence set, and predicts structured L1/L2/L3 outputs. Across proprietary systems, open LVLMs, MMD-Agent, and T2-Agent, ReMMD-Agent obtains the best five-way veracity performance, with 41.80% accuracy and 39.12% macro-F1 using GPT-5.2, while reducing cost by 17.5% relative to MMD-Agent and 79.9% relative to T2-Agent. The project is available at https://dang-ai.github.io/ReMMD.

</details>


### [167] [VeryTrace: Verifying Reasoning Traces through Compilable Formalism and Structured Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24124)
*Ninghan Zhong,Ahmet Ege Tanriverdi,Kaan Kale,Sriram Vishwanath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VeryTrace是一种零样本验证-修复框架，将自然语言推理轨迹形式化为可编译的结构化表示，通过混合验证器（确定性检查+LLM审计）实现步骤级错误定位与修复，在数学、机器人规划和亲属推理三个领域显著提升了准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chain-of-Thought多步推理存在错误传播问题：早期步骤的逻辑错误或幻觉会无声地传递，最终产生自信但错误的结论，亟需一种能够定位和修复推理步骤错误的方法。

Method: 提出VeryTrace框架：(1) 设计领域特定语言(DSL)，显式化步骤依赖关系、将定量内容转化为可执行表达式、通过演绎模式结构化语义推理；(2) 构建混合验证器，结合确定性检查（计算正确性、依赖解析、约束满足）和针对非机械化语义判断的LLM审计，实现步骤级错误定位与修复。

Result: 在竞赛数学(AIME 2025)、机器人规划(LLM-BabyBench)和亲属推理(CLUTRR)三个多样化领域，VeryTrace在零样本设置下提升了最先进LLM的准确性，无需领域特定训练或少样本示例。

Conclusion: 形式化的轨迹验证能够在实现高精度的同时保持跨领域的泛化能力，验证了结构化表示在增强LLM推理可靠性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-step reasoning with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting remains fragile: logical errors or hallucinations in early steps silently propagate, producing confident but incorrect conclusions. This paper presents VeryTrace, a zero-shot verification-and-repair framework that formalizes natural-language reasoning traces into a structured, compilable representation. VeryTrace introduces a Domain-Specific Language (DSL) that (i) makes step dependencies explicit, (ii) mechanizes quantitative content as executable expressions, and (iii) structures semantic inferences via deduction schemas. Our hybrid verifier combines deterministic checks for computational correctness, dependency resolution, and constraint satisfaction with targeted LLM audits for non-mechanizable semantic judgments, enabling step-level error localization and repair.
  Across three diverse domains-competition mathematics (AIME 2025), robotics planning (LLM-BabyBench), and kinship reasoning (CLUTRR), VeryTrace improves accuracy over zero-shot baselines on state-of-the-art LLMs without requiring domain-specific training or in-context examples, demonstrating that formalized trace verification achieves both precision and generalization.

</details>


### [168] [OmniPath: A Multi-Modal Agentic Framework for Auditing Wheelchair Accessibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24129)
*ASM Mobarak Hossain,Nadim Mahmud,Vaskar Raychoudhury,Md Osman Gani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniPath 是一个融合 OpenStreetMap 拓扑结构与高密度航空 LiDAR 数据的自动化环境审计系统，能够以亚米级精度分析人行道表面的运行坡度、横向坡度和垂直不连续性，依据 ADA 标准量化物理摩擦点并生成严重性评分（从“轻微”到“严重”），从而将静态地图转化为可预判无障碍挑战的动态数据源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于轮椅使用者而言，标准地图上的蓝色线条（无障碍路径标记）往往是“虚假承诺”——现有平台（如 OSM）能捕捉路径的位置，但无法传达实际通行的物理感受。这种信息障碍严重影响了轮椅使用者的出行决策与安全性。

Method: 将 OSM 的网络拓扑与 USGS 3DEP 高密度航空 LiDAR 数据融合，构建高保真三维行人环境模型。系统代理自动遍历道路网络，以 0.5 米为增量分析路面，量化运行坡度、横向坡度和垂直不连续性等物理摩擦点，依据 ADA 合规标准计算加权严重性评分，将危险等级分类为“轻微”至“严重”。验证采用分层随机抽样，在国家广场进行了 200 次实地地面真值调查。

Result: 系统对高严重性危险表现出较强的诊断可靠性：Severe 类别 F1-score 为 0.60，Critical 类别为 0.58。

Conclusion: OmniPath 实现了从被动地图到主动环境审计的范式转变，能够自动识别标准地图遗漏的“隐形”障碍，将静态数据集转化为无障碍数据源，使用户在家即可预判出行中的无障碍挑战。

Abstract: For a wheelchair user, a standard blue line on a map is often a broken promise. While platforms like OpenStreetMap (OSM) successfully capture where a path is, they frequently fail to convey how it physically feels to travel on it. This information barrier is problematic for wheelchair users. To solve this issue, we present OmniPath, a system that moves from passive mapping to proactive environmental auditing. Our framework fuses the network topology of OSM with the submeter precision of high-density aerial LiDAR (USGS 3DEP) to create a high-fidelity 3D model of the pedestrian environment. Rather than simply routing a user, our agent virtually traverses the network, analyzing the surface in 0.5 meter increments. It rigorously quantifies physical friction points specifically running slope, cross slope, and vertical discontinuities against ADA compliance standards, calculating a weighted severity score to categorize hazards from ``Mild'' to ``Critical.'' To ensure real world reliability, we validated the system against 200 physical ground truth field surveys across the National Mall using stratified random sampling. The framework demonstrated strong diagnostic reliability for high-severity hazards, achieving F1-scores of 0.60 for Severe and 0.58 for critical categories. By automating this micro-scale inspection, OmniPath identifies the ``invisible'' barriers that standard maps miss, effectively transforming a static dataset into accessibility data source that anticipates accessibility challenges before the user ever leaves home.

</details>


### [169] [T2D-Bench: Evidence-Gated Evaluation of LLM Outputs for Type 2 Diabetes Using a Multi-Layer Clinical-Lifestyle Knowledge Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24145)
*Saba A. Farahani,Hung Cao,Ramesh Jain,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出T2D-Bench基准和证据门控评估框架，用于检测LLM输出的2型糖尿病建议是否满足可计算的知识图谱证据要求。实验发现GPT-4o-mini和GPT-4o分别有35%和33%的基线输出未能通过证据路径检查，而证据门控机制可将未支持的遗漏显式化并通过约束修正使其合规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在2型糖尿病领域虽能生成临床流畅建议，但常违反指南约束或未能明确验证生活方式相关血糖声明的证据基础，缺乏系统性检测和修正这类问题的方法。

Method: 构建多层临床-生活方式知识图谱（融合UMLS、DrugBank、SIDER、ADA标准及生活方式-血糖机制桥接），基于此设计T2D-Bench基准和证据门控评估框架，在100个结构化场景（诊断、用药安全、对抗性生活方式冲突）上测试LLM输出。

Result: GPT-4o-mini和GPT-4o的基线输出分别有35%和33%未通过证据路径检查；证据门控能检测未支持的遗漏并通过约束修正使输出达到验证器级合规。

Conclusion: 可计算的证据约束能够将LLM在糖尿病输出中未支持的临床遗漏显式化、可度量且可纠正。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can produce clinically fluent recommendations for type 2 diabetes while failing to satisfy guideline constraints or explicitly justify lifestyle-related glycemic claims. We present T2D-Bench, a reproducible benchmark and evidence-gated evaluation framework for testing whether LLM outputs satisfy explicit, graph-checkable evidence requirements. T2D-Bench is built on a multi-layer clinical-lifestyle knowledge graph that combines a biomedical spine (UMLS, DrugBank, SIDER), computable ADA Standards of Care rules, and lifestyle knowledge connected through a mechanistic bridge to glycemic laboratory effects. Across 100 structured vignettes spanning diagnosis, medication safety, and adversarial lifestyle conflicts, baseline outputs failed benchmark-defined evidence-path checks in 35% of cases for GPT-4o-mini and 33% for GPT-4o. The evidence gate detects unsupported omissions and uses constrained revision to bring outputs into verifier-level compliance with benchmark-defined evidence requirements. These results show that computable evidence constraints can make unsupported clinical omissions explicit, measurable, and correctable in diabetes-focused LLM outputs.

</details>


### [170] [The Geometry Behind Diffusion and Flow Matching: Gradient Flows and Geodesics in Wasserstein Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24157)
*Yian Yao,Weiwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，概率测度空间 $\mathcal{P}_2(\mathbb{R}^d)$ 在 Wasserstein 距离下同时支撑两种变分原理：扩散模型对应自由能的梯度流（初值问题），最优传输流匹配对应 Wasserstein 测地线（边值问题）。扩散模型中的前向过程与 JKO 格式（DDPM/DDIM/NCSN/Energy Matching）均可统一为该几何框架下的自由能下降；流匹配则沿测地线做确定性 ODE 生成。二者路径不同但终点相同，从而在同一个黎曼流形上建立了精确的数学对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为扩散模型（如 DDPM、DDIM、NCSN）与最优传输流匹配（Flow Matching）提供统一的几何基础，澄清二者之间模糊的关系，并在 Wasserstein 流形上给出严格的数学解释。

Method: 将扩散模型解释为 Wasserstein 流形上自由能 $F(\rho) = KL(\rho \| \pi)$ 的梯度流（Fokker-Planck 方程），其 JKO 隐式欧拉离散化对应去噪步骤；将流匹配解释为同一流形上 Benamou-Brenier 公式的最小作用量测地线。通过这两种变分原理的对比，建立统一的框架。

Result: 扩散模型是初值问题（自由能梯度流），流匹配是边值问题（Wasserstein 测地线）；二者在同一流形上到达相同端点但路径不同。扩散模型路径弯曲、需要多步采样；最优传输流匹配路径为直线、可用少步确定性 ODE 生成。

Conclusion: Wasserstein 流形为生成模型提供了统一的几何语言：扩散与流匹配并非独立理论，而是同一几何结构下的两种不同变分路径。该视角有助于设计更高效的生成算法。

Abstract: The space $\mathcal{P}_2(\mathbb{R}^d$) of probability measures with finite second moment carries a natural geometry: the quadratic Wasserstein distance W_2 makes it a complete metric space and, following Otto, a (formal) Riemannian manifold whose geodesics are the optimal-transport interpolations. On this manifold, the gradient flow of the free energy F(rho) = KL(rho || π) is exactly the Fokker-Planck equation, and its implicit-Euler discretization is the JKO scheme. This is the geometry underlying diffusion models: the forward process descends the free energy, and each denoising step realizes one JKO step, which recovers DDPM, DDIM, NCSN/SMLD, and Energy Matching; this is one scheme, not separate theories. The same manifold supports a second variational principle. Its geodesics - the minimum-action curves of the Benamou-Brenier formula - are precisely the optimal-transport paths that Flow Matching learns. Fixing both endpoints and following the geodesic, generation becomes a deterministic ODE along a straight line, hence far fewer sampling steps. Placing both families of models on one manifold makes their relationship exact: diffusion follows a free-energy gradient flow, an initial-value problem; optimal-transport Flow Matching follows a Wasserstein geodesic, a boundary-value problem. The two reach the same endpoints along different paths.

</details>


### [171] [An Introduction to Causal Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24160)
*Elias Bareinboim,Junzhe Zhang,Sanghack Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出因果推断与强化学习尽管各自独立发展，但都基于反事实关系这一共同基础。通过将RL环境分解为结构因果模型，作者统一了在线学习、离线策略学习与因果学习等不同模式，并引入了广义策略学习、模仿学习和反事实学习等新任务，提出因果强化学习（CRL）这一交叉研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推断和强化学习这两个学科长期独立发展、几乎无交互，但两者都涉及反事实关系这一核心要素，作者希望明确建立二者之间的数学联系，从而催生新的学习范式。

Method: 将RL环境形式化为结构因果模型（SCM），将环境分解为具有不同因果不变性的自主机制集合，从而在统一框架下处理在线学习、离策略学习和因果计算学习等多种模式。

Result: 识别出文献中看似无关的学习模式之间的统一关系，并引入三类新的学习任务：广义策略学习（含干预）、模仿学习和反事实学习，提出因果强化学习（CRL）这一新领域。

Conclusion: 因果推断与强化学习的结合（CRL）具有巨大潜力，为反事实学习提供了更广阔的视角，值得深入探索。

Abstract: Causal inference provides a set of principles and tools that allow one to combine data and knowledge about an environment to reason with questions of counterfactual nature, i.e., what would have happened had reality been different, even when no data of this unrealized reality is currently available. Reinforcement learning provides methods to learn a policy that optimizes a specific measure (e.g., reward, regret) when the agent is deployed in an environment and pursues an exploratory, trial-and-error approach. These two disciplines have evolved independently and with virtually no interaction between them. We note that they operate over different aspects of the same building block, counterfactual relations, which makes them umbilically connected. Based on these observations, novel learning opportunities arise when this connection is explicitly acknowledged and mathematized. To realize this potential, we note that any environment where the RL agent is deployed can be decomposed as a collection of autonomous mechanisms with different causal invariances, parsimoniously modeled as a structural causal model; any standard RL setting implicitly encodes such a model. This formalization allows us to put under a unifying treatment different modes of learning, including online, off-policy, and causal calculus learning, which appear unrelated in the literature. However, these modalities are not exhaustive: we introduce several natural and pervasive classes of learning settings that entail novel dimensions of analysis. Specifically, we introduce and discuss through causal lenses generalized policy learning, where to intervene, imitation learning, and counterfactual learning. These tasks lead to a broader view of counterfactual learning and suggest great potential for studying causal inference and reinforcement learning side by side, which we call causal reinforcement learning (CRL).

</details>


### [172] [Data Scale, Not Latency, Shapes Cross-Lingual Encoder Transfer in Streaming ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24169)
*Nenad Banfic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过0.6B参数的FastConformer模型在8种欧洲语言上的系统实验，系统评估了多语言（ML）与仅英语（EN）编码器初始化对语音识别模型迁移的影响。结果表明：多语言初始化的优势仅体现在低数据量场景下，且随目标语言数据量增加而衰减（每翻倍数据量优势减半）；流式延迟不会放大该优势；4-bit权重量化在几乎不影响WER的情况下可将编码器占用减少约3倍。最终建议：低资源场景使用多语言初始化，高数据量场景无需区分，延迟和量化决策可独立进行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将流式语音识别模型适配到新语言时，存在多语言编码器和仅英语编码器两种热启动选择。常见的直觉认为多语言编码器在低数据量下更有优势，但该优势持续多久、流式延迟是否放大该优势、以及部署量化后是否仍然存在，这些问题尚不明确。本文旨在系统回答这些问题，为实际部署提供量化指导。

Method: 使用0.6B参数的缓存感知FastConformer换能器模型，在8种欧洲语言上进行控制实验。变量包括5个目标语言数据量级（100h到2500h）、3种流式延迟等级（含离线解码）和最多4个公开测试集。通过对比多语言与仅英语初始化在不同条件下的词错误率（WER）差异，并采用幂律拟合描述优势衰减规律。

Result: 在FLEURS测试集160ms延迟下，多语言与英语初始化的WER差距从100h时的+4.21个百分点降至2500h时的+0.20个百分点，呈幂律衰减（数据量翻倍优势减半）。三种流式延迟下，两种初始化的差距在100-1000h数据量间保持稳定，在2500h接近零。4-bit权重量化在560ms流式延迟下将编码器占用减少约3倍，平均WER增加约0.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 多语言初始化是一种数据受限优势而非延迟受限优势。建议：低数据量场景使用多语言初始化，大数据量场景两种初始化选择基本无关紧要，延迟和量化决策可独立于初始化选择进行。

Abstract: Adapting a streaming speech recognition model to a new language requires choosing between two plausible warm starts: a multilingual (ML) encoder or an English-only (EN) encoder. The common intuition is that the multilingual encoder should help most at low data, but it is unclear how long that advantage persists, whether tight streaming latency amplifies it, and whether it survives deployment quantization. We answer these questions with a controlled sweep of a 0.6 B-parameter cache-aware FastConformer transducer across eight European languages, up to five target-language data scales (100 h to 2500 h), three streaming tiers plus offline decoding, and up to four public test sets. The main result is that multilingual initialization is a data-limited advantage, not a latency-limited one. On FLEURS at 160 ms, the mean EN-ML word error rate (WER) gap falls from +4.21 percentage points (pp) at 100 h to +0.20 pp at 2500 h; a power-law fit summarizes this decay, with each doubling of target-language data roughly halving the remaining advantage. Across the three streaming tiers, the across-language mean EN-ML gap is approximately stable at each scale from 100 to 1000 h, and is near zero by 2500 h. Finally, 4-bit weight-only encoder quantization at the matched 560 ms streaming tier reduces the encoder footprint by about 3x, with an average FLEURS WER increase of about 0.5 pp. The resulting guideline is simple: use multilingual initialization in low-data regimes, treat the choice as effectively irrelevant at large data, and make latency and quantization decisions independently.

</details>


### [173] [Navigating User Behavior toward Personalized Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24196)
*Hengji Zhou,Yufeng Liu,Ye Liu,Yong Xu,Lianghao Xia,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Navigen通过双重标识（协同编码+文本编码）将用户交互行为转化为可执行的生成指令，结合两阶段SFT+RL训练框架，实现个性化图像与视频内容生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGC流程假设用户能提供完整创作指令，但用户很少能详细描述视觉细节，导致生成内容与用户需求不匹配。需要将用户交互历史转化为可执行的生成指令。

Method: 1) 双重标识表示：为每个物品分配协同编码（行为基础）和文本编码（语义桥梁）组成的双标识符；2) 两阶段SFT+RL流水线：先通过进化搜索监督蒸馏偏好推理和指令写作能力，再通过层次化自一致奖励使生成与用户意图对齐。

Result: 在产品、游戏和短视频三个领域，Navigen在个性化图像和视频生成、下一物品预测方面均有提升，能生成更具体、相关且可视觉生成的指令。

Conclusion: 提出了将用户行为编码为语言可推理形式并赋予模型指令写作能力的有效方案，为个性化内容生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Modern AIGC pipelines deliver high-fidelity images and videos but presuppose a well-formed creation instruction, while end users rarely articulate visual details, leaving generators misaligned with user demand. We study personalized content generation, which turns a user's interaction history into an executable instruction for downstream synthesis, and identify two obstacles: behavior must be encoded in a form legible to language reasoning, and the model must acquire instruction-writing skill absent from both pretraining and behavior data. We propose NaviGen, which represents each item with a dual identifier coupling a collaborative code and a textual code as a behavioral substrate and a semantic bridge in one token stream. On this representation, a two-stage SFT+RL pipeline first distills preference reasoning and instruction writing from evolutionarily searched supervision, then aligns generation with user intent through hierarchical and self-consistent rewards. Experiments across product, game, and short-video domains show that NaviGen improves personalized image and video generation, strengthens next-item prediction, and yields more specific, relevant, and visually generatable instructions. Our code is anonymously released at: https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/NaviGen.

</details>


### [174] [Exploring the relationship between human-centric AI and firm idiosyncratic risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24224)
*Zhen-Yuan Ralph Liu,Yu-Ting Wang,Jia-Jia Yan,Shivam Gupta,Mihalis Giannakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究工业5.0背景下人本AI（HCAI）对企业特质风险的影响，基于情境AI理论和社会技术系统理论，利用2015-2023年中国上市公司面板数据发现HCAI能降低企业特质风险，且数字化和高管持股强化该效应，而运营效率和IT背景CEO则削弱之。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管工业5.0中关于人本AI的讨论广泛，但其对企业特质风险的影响尚未被充分探索，而这一议题对于企业在技术革命中管理财务风险至关重要，因为特质风险反映了投资者对企业异质性AI战略和实施的反应。

Method: 整合情境AI理论与社会技术系统理论，将HCAI概念化为一种情境化AI策略，采用2015-2023年中国上市公司多源面板数据集进行实证分析。

Result: HCAI与企业较低的特质风险相关；数字化和高管持股增强风险降低效应，而运营效率和IT背景CEO意外地削弱该效应。

Conclusion: 研究结果为AI时代的伦理AI治理和企业财务风险管理提供了理论贡献和实践见解。

Abstract: Despite the extensive discussions of human-centric AI (HCAI) in Industry 5.0, its effects on firms' idiosyncratic risks (IR) remains underexplored. This is an imperative issue for firms navigate financial risks during the current technological revolution, as IR reflects investor reactions to corporate heterogeneous AI strategies and implementations by isolating firm-level stock volatility from systematic factors. Integrating situated AI theory with social-technical systems theory, we conceptualise HCAI as a situated AI strategy that reduces AI-related ethical risks and fosters AI-Human synergies in firms' business operations, ultimately reducing IR by aligning with stakeholders' diverse expectations. Moreover, socio-technical factors, namely digitalisation, operational efficiency, executive shareholding, and CEOs with IT background, may moderate the HCAI-IR relationship. Using a multi-source panel dataset of Chinese listed firms from 2015 to 2023, we find that HCAI is associated with lower firm IR. Furthermore, digitalisation and executive shareholding strengthen this risk-reducing effect, whereas operational efficiency and CEOs with IT background surprisingly attenuate it. Our findings offer theoretical contributions and practical insights for both ethical AI governance and firm financial risk management in the AI era.

</details>


### [175] [FlowR2A: Learning Reward-to-Action Distribution for Multimodal Driving Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24231)
*Xirui Li,Zhe Liu,Xiaoqing Ye,Wenhua Han,Yifeng Pan,Junyu Han,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowR2A 提出通过流匹配解码器将仿真奖励从判别目标重构为生成条件，从而统一了基于评分方法的密集监督和基于锚点方法的提案生成，在 NAVSIM v1/v2 基准上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多模态驾驶规划中基于评分方法（密集奖励监督但受限于固定动作词汇）与基于锚点方法（动态生成提案但受限于单一真实轨迹的稀疏监督）之间的长期张力。

Method: 将仿真奖励重构为生成条件，通过学习奖励条件下的动作分布（基于密集轨迹-奖励对的流匹配解码器）；引入细粒度每时间步奖励条件和奖励噪声增强；支持测试时通过奖励引导和锚定采样的可控采样。

Result: 在 NAVSIM v1 和 v2 基准上取得 SOTA 结果，生成的多模态提案质量显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: FlowR2A 通过生成式框架统一了两类规划范式的优势，使模型内化动作与安全性、进展、舒适性、规则合规性结果之间的相关性，并实现了高质量的可控提案生成。

Abstract: Multimodal driving planning faces a long-standing tension between two paradigms: scoring-based methods benefit from dense reward supervision but are confined to a fixed action vocabulary, while anchor-based methods generate proposals dynamically yet suffer from sparse supervision constrained to a single ground-truth trajectory. In this work, we propose FlowR2A, which resolves this tension by reframing simulation-based rewards from discriminative targets into generative conditions. By learning the reward-conditioned action distribution from dense trajectory-reward pairs with a flow-matching decoder, FlowR2A unifies the dense supervision of scoring-based methods with the proposal generation of anchor-based methods in a single generative model, forcing the model to internalize the correlation between an action and its outcomes in safety, progress, comfort, and rule compliance. To balance hard safety constraints against soft progress objectives, we introduce fine-grained per-timestep reward conditioning and reward noise augmentation. The generative formulation naturally supports controllable test-time sampling via reward guidance and anchored sampling, producing high-quality proposals. FlowR2A achieves state-of-the-art results on the NAVSIM v1 and v2 benchmarks, with multimodal proposals of substantially higher quality than prior methods.

</details>


### [176] [SP-Mind: An Autonomous Reasoning Agent for Spatial Proteomics Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24235)
*Yucheng Yuan,Yuanfeng Ji,Zhongxiao Li,Ruijiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SP-Mind是首个统一空间蛋白质组学分析管线的自主AI智能体，可将自然语言查询转化为端到端分析流程，在SP-Bench基准测试中达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前空间蛋白质组学分析流程碎片化，依赖专家手动编排异构工具，限制了研究的可扩展性和可重复性。

Method: 构建了SP-Mind自主AI智能体，配备专家策划的生物分析技能和专用计算工具，将自然语言查询转化为端到端分析流程，无需任务特定微调；同时构建了SP-Bench综合基准（涵盖多种组织类型，102个任务，18个类别）。

Result: 在SP-Bench和既定下游任务上的广泛评估表明，SP-Mind相比现有开源生物医学智能体基线取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: SP-Mind成功实现了空间蛋白质组学分析管线的统一和自动化，提升了研究的可扩展性和可重复性。

Abstract: Spatial proteomics enables single-cell-resolution characterization of protein expression within tissue architecture, playing a critical role in understanding tumor microenvironments and guiding precision medicine. However, current analysis workflows remain fragmented, requiring expert manual orchestration of heterogeneous tools and limiting research scalability and reproducibility. We present SP-Mind, the first autonomous AI agent designed to unify the spatial proteomics analysis pipeline, from raw multiplexed tissue imaging to downstream phenotype discovery. Equipped with expert-curated biological analysis skills and specialized computational tools, SP-Mind converts natural-language queries into end-to-end analytical workflows without task-specific fine-tuning. To rigorously evaluate its capabilities, we introduce SP-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark spanning diverse tissue types, comprising 102 tasks across 18 distinct categories. Through extensive evaluation on SP-Bench and established downstream tasks, SP-Mind achieves state-of-the-art performance compared to existing open-source biomedical agent baselines.

</details>


### [177] [Towards Federated Long-Tailed Graph Learning: An Energy-Guided Dual Decoupling Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24237)
*Lianshuai Guo,Zhongzheng Yuan,Xunkai Li,Meixia Qu,Wenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对联邦图学习中的长尾分布问题，提出FedEPD框架，通过拓扑净化与语义重校准的双解耦范式，利用Dirichlet能量剪枝过滤异嗜边、从拓扑中心节点提取全局原型进行空间低通注入，并采用两阶段交替优化策略，在多种长尾基准上实现SOTA性能，准确率提升达4.97%，Macro-F1提升达5.48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据类别常呈现长尾分布，这种统计稀缺性从两方面严重降低性能：使全局模型偏向多数类，以及将少数节点淹没在异嗜性的头部主导邻域中导致结构隔离。现有拓扑无关的统计补偿方法在数据稀缺下容易过拟合相邻主导类的结构噪声，导致表示退化。

Method: 提出FedEPD框架，基于双解耦范式分离拓扑净化与语义重校准：(1) 利用分布感知的Dirichlet能量剪枝过滤空间异嗜边；(2) 从拓扑中心节点提取鲁棒的全局原型，通过空间低通原型注入融入局部表示；(3) 采用两阶段交替优化策略，严格保护多数类决策边界的同时提升少数类精度。

Result: 在多种长尾基准上达到SOTA性能，准确率绝对提升4.97%，Macro-F1绝对提升5.48%。

Conclusion: FedEPD通过拓扑与语义的双重解耦策略，有效解决了联邦图学习中长尾分布带来的统计稀缺和结构隔离问题，验证了该范式在分布式图建模场景下的优越性。

Abstract: Federated Graph Learning facilitates collaborative graph modeling across distributed clients while preserving data privacy. However, real-world data categories frequently exhibit long-tailed distributions. Such statistical scarcity severely degrades performance in two ways: it biases the global model toward majority classes, and it structurally isolates minority nodes by submerging them in heterophilic, head-dominated neighborhoods. While existing methods attempt topology-agnostic statistical compensations, they often fail under data scarcity. Instead of recovering tail nodes, they overfit the structural noise from adjacent dominant classes, leading to representation degradation. To address these limitations, we propose FedEPD, a framework built on a dual decoupling paradigm that separates topological purification from semantic recalibration. Specifically, FedEPD utilizes distribution-aware Dirichlet energy pruning to filter spatial heterophilic edges. It then overcomes Non-IID distribution shifts by extracting robust global prototypes from topologically central nodes, which are incorporated into local representations via a spatial low-pass prototype injection. Furthermore, a two stage alternating optimization strategy strictly protects majority decision boundaries while improving minority accuracy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FedEPD achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse long-tailed benchmarks, yielding absolute improvements of up to 4.97% in Accuracy and 5.48% in Macro-F1.

</details>


### [178] [Probing the Misaligned Thinking Process of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24251)
*Kaiwen Zhou,Constantin Venhoff,Jonathan Michala,Xin Eric Wang,William Saunders*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过将LLM中的失调行为分解为细粒度认知过程（失调指标），并用线性探针检测模型内部激活中的这些指标，从而实现对失调行为的可靠监控。在5种失调行为上，该方法在OOD基准上达到0.935 AUROC，同时保持对正常对话的低误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型表现出日益增多的失调行为（如策略性欺骗、装弱、自我保存），且被部署在高风险场景中，因此需要可靠地检测这些行为以确保安全负责的使用。

Method: 将失调行为分解为18个细粒度失调指标，构建自动化的元计划引导流水线生成多轮训练对话，通过线性探针检测模型内部激活中的指标存在性，并使用OOD测试套件（含自动化行为诱发、现有失调基准和自然良性对话）进行泛化评估。

Result: 在5种失调行为上，线性探针在OOD基准上达到0.935 AUROC，与强LLM评判相当，且在良性流量上保持低假阳性率。

Conclusion: 通过分解失调行为为可检测的认知指标并使用内部激活进行监控，是一种有效且泛化能力强的失调检测方法。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit a growing range of misaligned behaviors such as strategic deception, sandbagging, and self-preservation. As they are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings, it is critical to reliably detect such behaviors to ensure safe and responsible use.
